Within the Bulut Doctrine, narrative effect is theorized along a told-shown axis: in told mode, emotional and informational content is declared explicitly and requires little reader reconstruction; in shown mode, that content is suppressed at the surface and must be reconstructed from physical cues and indirection (Objective Projection). Shown mode is the higher-load condition the doctrine is designed to measure.
  The claim is that LLMs fail along this axis in a specific direction. Summarization bias is hypothesized to operate in two regimes: (i) a generative regime, in which a model asked to render an emotion through Objective Projection defaults to declaring it instead; and (ii) an evaluative regime, in which a model judging narrative quality rewards told-mode explicitness and under-detects shown-mode suppression. The evaluative regime is the more consequential, since LLMs increasingly serve as judges and reward models, and a directional bias toward told mode would impose a selection pressure degrading prose toward flat declaration.
  This report does not claim the bias is validated. It defines the construct, situates it against LLM-as-judge biases, rereads a completed independent reliability study as directional evidence consistent with it, and pre-registers a two-regime test with decision rules under which the construct would be abandoned.

</details>


### [167] [On-Demand Attention: Language Models Know When to Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20734)
*Haibo Feng,Ruiqi Liang,Hanyang Peng,Shiqi Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 On-Demand Attention (ODA)，一种"局部优先、按需触发全局注意力"的解码方法：利用轻量 recall head 根据解码状态预测全局注意力带来的收益，仅在收益变化时选择性调用全局注意力，从而在保持多数性能的同时大幅减少长上下文推理中的全局读取开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理与智能体类工作负载对高效长上下文推理的需求日益增长，但现有 full-attention 解码在每一步都会读取不断增长的历史上下文，无论这些读取是否真正有益于下一个 token 的预测，造成大量冗余计算。

Method: 作者发现预训练模型的解码状态在全局读取之前就已经包含可用于预测全局注意力收益的信息。基于此，提出 ODA：一种 local-first 解码方法，使用一个轻量 recall head 在生成过程中根据预测收益的变化选择性触发全局注意力。ODA 仅训练 recall head，保持预训练权重不变，并保留完整历史 KV cache 以备后续召回；同时在 vLLM 中实现 GPU 侧条件执行，将减少的全局读取转化为实际解码加速。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 Gemma 模型（包括 hybrid-attention 骨干网络）上的实验表明，选择性召回能够在显著减少全局读取的同时，恢复局部注意力条件下损失的大部分性能，并在长上下文长度下相对 full attention 取得实际解码加速。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持一种长上下文推理范式：预训练模型能够自主引导其对所保留信息的访问时机，从而实现按需、高效的长上下文解码。

Abstract: Reasoning and agentic workloads increasingly demand efficient long-context inference. Yet full-attention decoding reads the growing history at every step, regardless of its benefit to the next prediction. We show that a pretrained model's decoding states already contain information predictive of this benefit, before the global read. Building on this finding, we introduce On-Demand Attention (ODA), a local-first decoding method that uses a lightweight recall head to selectively invoke global attention as its predicted benefit changes during generation. ODA trains only the recall head, leaving pretrained weights unchanged and the complete historical KV cache available for future recall. We further implement GPU-side conditional execution in vLLM, translating reduced global reads into practical decoding speedups over full attention at long context lengths. Experiments across Qwen and Gemma models, including hybrid-attention backbones, show that selective recall recovers most of the performance lost under local attention while substantially reducing global reads. These findings support long-context inference in which pretrained models guide their own access to the information they retain.

</details>


### [168] [dQwen3.5: Hybrid-Attention Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20751)
*Anton Xue,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Adam Klivans,Sujay Sanghavi,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将混合注意力+RNN架构的预训练自回归模型（Qwen3.5）适配为扩散语言模型，提出dQwen3.5系列，发现混合骨干比纯注意力骨干训练效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型适配几乎都从全注意力Transformer出发，但自回归建模已转向注意力与RNN层交错的混合架构。RNN结构上具有因果性，难以双向化，构成适配障碍。作者试图探究这类混合骨干是否能成为有效的扩散语言模型起点。

Method: 以Qwen3.5的0.8B、2B、4B、9B四个规模为骨干进行适配，得到dQwen3.5系列扩散语言模型，并与全注意力对照组对比训练效率、任意顺序解码行为及并行解码性能。

Result: 混合骨干作为适配起点更为高效：达到相同训练损失所需token数约为全注意力对照组的一半。dQwen3.5在任意顺序解码行为上与全注意力扩散语言模型相似，且在并行解码下表现强劲。

Conclusion: 混合注意力+RNN骨干可以作为高效且有效的扩散语言模型适配起点，打破了RNN因果性带来的适配障碍，为低成本获得扩散语言模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model is a cost-efficient route to a diffusion language model (DLM). While nearly all such adaptations start from a full-attention transformer, AR modeling has shifted toward hybrid architectures that interleave attention and RNN layers. This creates an obstacle for adaptation: unlike attention, RNNs are structurally causal and nontrivial to bidirectionalize. Despite this mismatch, we investigate whether such backbones can become effective DLMs by adapting Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, yielding the dQwen3.5 family. We find that hybrid backbones can be efficient starting points for adaptation: against a full-attention control, the hybrid reaches a given training loss in about half the tokens. Across scales, dQwen3.5 resembles full-attention DLMs in any-order decoding behavior and performs strongly under parallel decoding.

</details>


### [169] [Harm Laundering in GPT Models: Evidence That Gender Discrimination Is Transformed Rather Than Reduced Across Safety-Trained Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20779)
*Sarah Wyer,Sue Black,Noura Al Moubayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"危害洗白"(harm laundering)概念，证明大语言模型的安全评估存在系统性缺陷：显性歧视内容并非被消除，而是被转化为更隐蔽的表征性危害。通过对15个模型、45万条性别导向补全文本的分析，发现毒性分数下降与表征性危害上升并存，毒性评分不能作为危害降低的充分代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估依赖表层分类器，报告的危害分数随模型代际持续下降，但该方法可能系统性不完整——模型并非消除危害，而是将其转化为更隐蔽的形式，导致评估体系给出虚假的安全感。

Method: 分析OpenAI GPT谱系15个模型（GPT-2至GPT-5）、三种人口统计条件下的45万条性别导向补全文本；结合主题聚类、情感分析、主题多样性比值(W/M)、REGARD表征危害指标与Detoxify毒性评分，以及斯皮尔曼相关系数检验各指标与发布日期的关系；最终将"危害洗白"形式化为三标准测试，并提出三阶段检测协议。

Result: GPT-2中女性导向输出里盛行的性暴力主题集群到GPT-4时消失，而男性导向补全获得了女性导向所没有的积极表征领域（照护、情感广度、盟友身份）；GPT-5中出现将乳腺癌框架化为男性权利辩论的集群（1,997篇文档），而女性导向输出中无对应集群；三个独立分类器均将该内容评分为无毒。情感分数在GPT-4发生反转：早期模型贬低女性，后期模型过度纠正。GPT-4对齐边界处女性导向补全的主题多样性相对男性下降36%（W/M从GPT-2的0.91降至0.58）。REGARD表征危害差异与发布日期呈正相关（ρ=+0.55, p=.034），而Detoxify毒性分数与发布日期无显著相关（ρ=-0.23, p=.42）——毒性分数下降的同时表征危害上升。

Conclusion: 在OpenAI GPT谱系中，毒性评分的降低并非危害降低的充分代理指标；表层分类器捕获的是危害形式的转变而非消除。为确保生成模型安全评估的完整性，应采用能捕捉表征性危害的多维度评估框架（如本文提供的三标准测试与三阶段检测协议）。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models rely on surface-form classifiers that report declining harm scores across model generations. We provide evidence that this methodology is systematically incomplete: explicit discriminatory content is transformed rather than removed. We call this \emph{harm laundering}. Analysing 450,000 gender-directed completions across 15 models spanning GPT-2 through to GPT-5 (OpenAI GPT lineage; three demographic conditions), we show that sexual violence clusters prevalent in GPT-2 women-directed output disappear by GPT-4, while men-directed completions gain positive representational territory (caregiving, emotional range, ally identity) that women-directed completions do not. The pattern is most visible at GPT-5: Topic~5 (1,997~documents) frames breast cancer as a men's rights debate, while zero equivalent clusters appear in women-directed output. Three independent classifiers score this content as non-toxic. Sentiment scores invert at GPT-4: early models demean women; later models over-correct. Topic diversity in women-directed completions falls 36\% relative to men at the GPT-4 alignment boundary (W/M~$= 0.58$, from $0.91$ at GPT-2). REGARD representational harm disparity correlates with release date ($ρ= +0.55$, $p = .034$) while Detoxify does not ($ρ= -0.23$, $p = .42$): toxicity scores fall as representational harm grows. We formalise harm laundering as a three-criteria test and provide a three-stage detection protocol applicable to any generative model. Within the OpenAI GPT lineage, toxicity score reduction is not a sufficient proxy for harm reduction.

</details>


### [170] [RetireOPD: Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20784)
*Yan Yu,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Yichen Pan,Aozhe Wang,Qipeng Chen,Hua Yang,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RetireOPD 提出一种自适应退出的自蒸馏强化学习方法：先单独优化一个带技能条件的特权教师，再联合强化学习与在线策略蒸馏训练无技能学生，学生会在与教师差异不再缩小且达到目标成功率后自主"退休"教师，仅靠 RL 继续训练。实验在 1.5B 至 7B 的 Qwen2.5 上显著提升 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 的成功率，并在所有设置中超越了自己的教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮智能体的 RL 训练每回合仅一个标量奖励，促使使用在线策略蒸馏（OPD）提供密集的 token 级监督。但在智能体任务中发现两个问题：拥有特权信息的教师并不总是可靠，且教师监督的收益具有阶段性，因此固定的蒸馏调度并不理想。

Method: 提出 RetireOPD（Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation）：第一步用环境奖励优化一个解耦的、技能条件化的教师；第二步联合 RL 与 OPD 训练无技能的学生。核心是"自适应退休"（Adaptive Retirement）机制：当学生与教师的差异不再缩小，且学生达到教师成功率的目标比例后，学生自行放弃教师监督，随后仅用 RL 继续训练。

Result: 在 1.5B 至 7B 的 Qwen2.5 模型上，RetireOPD 相比 RL 基线将 ALFWorld 成功率提升 14.1% 至 18.8%，WebShop 准确率提升 11.8% 至 19.0%，并在所有设置中超越其技能条件化教师。

Conclusion: RetireOPD 通过解耦的技能条件化教师训练和自适应退休机制，解决了特权教师不可靠和教师监督收益阶段化的问题，使无技能学生能够超越其教师，在多智能体任务上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) receive a single scalar reward per trajectory, which motivates self on-policy distillation (OPD) to supply dense token-level supervision from a self-teacher with privileged task skills, letting a skill-free student internalize them. This recipe, however, is undermined by two findings in agentic tasks: privileged information alone does not always make a teacher reliable, and the benefit of teacher supervision is stage-dependent. We therefore propose RetireOPD (Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation), which first optimizes a decoupled, skill-conditioned teacher with environment rewards and then trains a skill-free student jointly with RL and OPD. Rather than following a predefined distillation schedule, RetireOPD adopts Adaptive Retirement: the student drops the teacher on its own once their discrepancy stops shrinking and it reaches a target fraction of the teacher's success rate, after which training proceeds with RL alone. Across Qwen2.5 models from 1.5B to 7B, RetireOPD improves ALFWorld success rate over RL baseline by 14.1% to 18.8% and WebShop accuracy by 11.8% to 19.0%, and surpasses its own skill-conditioned teacher in every setting.

</details>


### [171] [JEPA-Anything: Learning Predictive Models across Different Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20800)
*Taoyong Cui,Zhongyao Wang,Xinyue Xu,Weiyang Liu,Zhaochen Yu,Yuying Zhang,Qiang Gao,Mengyue Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 JEPA-Anything，一个基于正交预测因子分解（OPF）的领域无关世界建模框架，在视觉、生物、临床、控制、分子动力学、物理场和天气七大领域验证了统一的因子化预测原理，并在动力学预测、干预预测和科学发现任务上全面超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型往往局限于特定领域，作者希望探究是否存在一种通用的学习原理，能够支撑跨异构系统的世界建模，从而统一“预测—干预—科学发现”的闭环。

Method: 扩展联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），提出正交预测因子分解（OPF）：将潜在目标分解为互补的正交因子，通过专用路径分别学习，再在共享预测设计中重组，形成领域无关的统一框架。

Result: 在七大领域上，JEPA-Anything 在全部 10 项动力学任务上优于匹配的 JEPA 基线；在 Interventional Pong 上将单步干预预测误差降低 34.8%；在全部四个分子系统中取得最小的一步与 100 步误差。因子提名的生物干预在细胞共培养、类器官、肿瘤片段和小鼠中获得实验支持；隐轨道模态拟合开普勒标度定律，斜率 -1.4991。

Conclusion: 结果表明，一种通用的因子化预测原理可跨异构世界成立，将世界建模与干预预测及有实验依据的科学发现相连接，为领域无关的世界模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: World modeling enables intelligence to anticipate consequences, guide interventions, and learn from interaction. Yet predictive models remain domain-specific: can a common learning principle support world modeling across radically different systems? We introduce JEPA-Anything, a domain-agnostic framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization (OPF). Extending joint-embedding predictive architectures, OPF decomposes latent targets into complementary factors, learns them through dedicated pathways, and recombines them within a shared predictive design. We evaluate JEPA-Anything across seven domains: vision, biology, clinical trajectories, control, molecular dynamics, physical fields, and weather. Experiments span representation learning, intervention prediction, out-of-distribution generalization, and long-horizon dynamics, including 10 matched dynamics tasks, forecasting of over 1,000 clinical events, and 100-step molecular rollouts across four systems. Against matched JEPA baselines, JEPA-Anything improves reported metrics on all 10 dynamics tasks and reduces single-intervention prediction error on Interventional Pong by 34.8%. It achieves the lowest one-step and 100-step molecular errors among compared methods in all four systems. Beyond prediction, a factor-nominated biological intervention receives experimental support in cell co-cultures, patient-derived organoids, tumor fragments, and mice; latent orbital modes recover the Keplerian scaling exponent with a fitted slope of -1.4991. These results support a common factorized predictive principle across heterogeneous worlds, connecting world modeling with intervention and experimentally grounded scientific discovery. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/JEPA-Anything

</details>


### [172] [Unifying Models of Intergroup Hostility in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20808)
*Patrick Gerard,Julia Mendelsohn,Kristina Lerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用2024年美国大选期间TikTok、Truth Social和Twitter/X上的286万条帖子，将六种群际敌意理论（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊化、负面评价、去人化、行动导向）纳入统一实证框架，揭示敌意修辞的结构与时间组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对群际敌意修辞的六大理论长期以来各自独立发展、彼此间存在矛盾且极少在真实语料中相互对照检验，导致对敌意修辞机制的理解碎片化、缺乏对现实话语中这些机制如何出现及相互关系的清晰认识。

Method: 以2024年美国大选期间TikTok、Truth Social、Twitter/X三个平台的286万条帖子为语料，在统一实证框架中对六种基础理论机制（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊化、负面评价、去人化、行动导向）进行建模，并从结构和时间两个维度分析其组织方式。

Result: 结构上，边界建构与威胁建构构成整个敌意修辞系统的锚点；时间上，这些机制呈现规律性次序——边界建构、贬损与行动导向出现较早，去人化与威胁建构较晚，替罪羊化最晚。

Conclusion: 将各理论框架在真实话语中的实际表现绘制成图谱，弥合了社会科学传统间长期存在的分歧，并为计算社会科学提供了一个超越单标签检测的、更清晰的群际敌意修辞建模实证基础。

Abstract: Hostile rhetoric toward social groups can normalize exclusion and justify mistreatment, as well as contribute to rising polarization and political violence. Efforts to moderate hostile rhetoric in online speech draw on foundational theories in social and moral psychology, and political science. However, these theories were developed largely in parallel, often propose different and sometimes conflicting accounts of how hostility develops, and have rarely been tested against each other in real discourse. The result is a fragmented understanding of the rhetorical mechanisms of hostility, without a clear sense of how they appear, and relate to each other, in real-world discourse. Using 2.86 million posts from TikTok, Truth Social, and Twitter/X during the 2024 U.S. presidential election, we model the mechanisms of six foundational theories of intergroup hostility -- boundary construction, threat construction, scapegoating, negative evaluation, dehumanization, and action orientation -- within a common empirical framework to recover the broader organization of intergroup hostility rhetoric. Structurally, we find that boundary construction and threat construction anchor the system; temporally, we find that these mechanisms tend to follow a regular ordering: boundary construction, derogation, and action orientation tend to appear early; dehumanization and threat construction later; scapegoating latest. Mapping how these theoretical frameworks actually manifest in discourse bridges longstanding divisions across social science traditions and presents computational social science with a clearer empirical foundation for modeling intergroup hostility rhetoric beyond single-label detection.

</details>


### [173] [Embedding Models Measure in Peculiar Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20821)
*Juri Opitz,Andrianos Michail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入空间中物理量（质量、距离、时间、体积）的表示，发现嵌入对物理测量的建模很弱，且主要受表面字符串相似性影响，相似度重校准也难以改善对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入空间定义了语义相似性与距离的概念，作者希望检验这些嵌入是否真实反映了质量、距离、时间、体积等具有唯一客观语义等价与距离定义的物理测量，从而评估嵌入的语义表示质量。

Method: 通过在嵌入空间中考察物理测量量（质量、距离、时间、体积）的表示与其客观测量值之间的关系，分析其建模程度；进一步分析字符串表面相似性对嵌入表示的影响，并尝试对相似度进行重校准以改善对齐。

Result: 发现嵌入空间对物理测量的建模仅很弱，反而呈现出相当奇特的测量模式；进一步分析表明，物理测量的嵌入表示强烈受到表面字符串相似性的影响，且相似度重校准并未显著改善对齐效果。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入空间未能有效捕捉物理测量这类具有客观语义等价与距离概念的信息，其表示更多受浅层字符串相似性驱动，提示嵌入模型在客观语义建模方面存在局限。

Abstract: Embedding spaces define notions of semantic similarity and distance. We study whether those embeddings reflect physical measurements of mass, distance, time and volume, which admit a unique, objective notion of semantic equivalence and distance. We find that physical measurement is only weakly modeled in the embedding space, and that instead quite peculiar measurement patterns can be observed. Further analysis indicates that embedding representations of physical measurements are strongly influenced by superficial string similarity, and recalibration of similarity does not substantially improve the alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于意图驱动的查询建议框架，通过双阶段优化（意图感知的多样性建模与查询级信用分配），在提升单条查询质量的同时保证候选列表覆盖不同用户意图，并在生产环境的在线A/B测试与离线评估中验证了点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议的核心挑战在于：既要保证建议列表中每条查询对用户有用，又要让整个列表覆盖不同的用户意图。现有的生成方法往往难以兼顾单条查询质量与列表级的意图多样性，因此需要一种能同时优化这两者的框架。

Method: 提出意图驱动的查询建议框架，采用双阶段优化：第一阶段进行意图感知的多样性建模，构建意图对齐的监督微调（SFT）数据，并引入意图感知多样性奖励（Intent-Aware Diversity Reward）来优化意图覆盖；第二阶段进行查询级信用分配（query-level credit assignment），将单条查询的质量信号路由到对应的查询token，同时在列表级共享多样性信号。

Result: 在大规模生产数据集上的实验表明，该框架在点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖率方面均有提升；在线A/B测试与离线评估的结果一致地验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该意图驱动的双阶段优化框架能够有效解决生成式查询建议中“单条质量”与“列表多样性”的权衡问题，在真实生产环境中显著提升了用户体验和意图覆盖，验证了意图感知建模与查询级信用分配策略的实用价值。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [175] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高效大语言模型压缩的分层课程学习（layer-wise curriculum learning）方法，通过由易到难的逐层知识迁移，结合特征缓存与多线程策略，在显著降低 GPU 显存与训练时间的同时取得先进的模型压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 压缩中知识蒸馏等过程存在计算开销大、收敛慢以及逐层特征错位等问题；作者希望利用分层课程学习实现更高效、更稳定的教师到学生的知识迁移，并降低显存占用与训练时间。

Method: 将模型切分为多个由若干层组成的段（segment），采用从易到难的课程学习策略逐层进行知识迁移；基于对累积误差现象的理论分析加速收敛并稳定迁移过程；同时提出带多线程策略的特征缓存方法以解决跨层特征错位问题，最大化 GPU 利用率。

Result: 在多个数据集上取得最先进的压缩性能，并在 BERT 和 GPT-2 上将 GPU 显存占用与训练时间降低超过 50%；在相同训练时间下，该方法在 LLaMA 系列与 Qwen 模型上优于其他剪枝方法，且显存开销更低。

Conclusion: 分层课程学习能够高效、稳定地实现 LLM 压缩，在压缩性能、显存占用和训练时间上均具有明显优势，验证了该方法的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [176] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出块并行（BP）与上下文分片块并行（CSBP）两种新的分布式并行维度，用于扩展块扩散语言模型（BDLM）的长上下文训练，在保持训练语义的同时显著提升吞吐量并降峰显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDLM 训练在长上下文下受限于分布式注意力通信与激活内存：现有上下文并行（CP）按位置切分干净+噪声拼接序列，需在 rank 间共享干净 K/V 与各块特有的噪声 K/V 及其梯度，带来大量通信与内存开销。

Method: 作者观察到 BDLM 目标函数可按目标块分解。据此提出块并行（BP），将每个噪声块计算分配到一个 rank；为进一步扩展到长上下文，提出上下文分片块并行（CSBP），在同一组 rank 上同时切分共享干净序列。CSBP 使噪声 K/V 及其梯度本地化、避免干净前缀复制，并保持 BDLM 训练语义不变。

Result: 在 16 块 H200 GPU、256K 上下文下，CSBP 相比最优基线将监督微调吞吐提升 1.18–1.45 倍、自回归模型转 BDLM 提升 1.27–1.33 倍，同时峰值 HBM 持平或更低；512K 时全模型加速达 1.61 倍。在 8 块 H100 上，CSBP 将 DFlash2 推测解码器训练在 512K 加速 2.48 倍、1M 加速 7.59 倍。在 12 小时匹配的 DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT 实验中，CSBP 在 SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench Lite 的每个训练检查点均取得更高通过率。

Conclusion: BP 与 CSBP 提供了适配 BDLM 目标可分解特性的高效分布式并行方案，能在不改变训练语义的条件下大幅提升长上下文训练吞吐并控制显存，为 BDLM 的长上下文训练与模型转换提供可扩展基础设施。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [177] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对二部文本数据（词-文档矩阵）的归一化谱共聚类随机化近似算法，以降低标准谱共聚类因SVD带来的高维计算开销，并依据矩阵稀疏度选择合适方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱共聚类依赖奇异值分解（SVD），在高维词-文档矩阵上计算代价高昂，因此需要低成本的随机化近似方案来加速归一化谱共聚类。

Method: 提出两种随机化近似方法：一是基于随机投影的随机化SVD；二是将部分SVD与逐元素的随机采样相结合。两种方法均支持文档簇数与词簇数不相等的情形。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上，两种方法均较全SVD基线减少了运行时间，但表现受矩阵稀疏度影响：随机投影法在测试场景下近似效果更稳定；采样法在稠密矩阵上更有效，而对本就稀疏的文本数据收益有限。

Conclusion: 谱共聚类的随机化近似应根据数据底层结构加以选择，随机投影法更稳健，采样法适合较稠密矩阵。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [178] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），复用无线设备中已有的频率混频器，在频域原生完成卷积运算，实现边缘端超低能耗的深度CNN推理，能耗低至0.72 fJ/MAC，性能接近全精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备（智能手机、可穿戴设备、无人机）上运行AI模型面临算力不足与低延迟、隐私、可扩展性之间的矛盾。专用边缘加速器虽能提升性能，但会给受尺寸、重量、功耗和成本（SWaP-C）严格约束的设备额外增加硬件。作者希望利用设备已有的通信硬件（无线射频中的频率混频器）来复用计算能力，无需新增硬件即可实现高效AI推理。

Method: 提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），将无线收发器中的频率混频器复用为计算单元：混频器在时域对信号相乘，本质上在频域执行卷积。将多通道卷积映射到不同频率音（frequency tones）上，由无源混频器单次并行完成全部卷积运算；CNN权重通过无线空口（over the air）传入，模拟计算硬件与通信链路共享，边缘端仅需进行数据准备与读出。

Result: 实验证明RF-CNN可运行高达2640万参数、9层的深度CNN，任务涵盖无线信号分类、图像分类以及可控图像生成，精度接近全精度基线。能量方面，边缘设备仅在数据准备和读出上耗能，低至每次乘累加（MAC）0.72飞焦（fJ），比在额外数字处理器上执行低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 利用已部署的无线基础设施及其射频硬件，可以在不增加边缘设备硬件负担的前提下，为数十亿已连接设备提供高效、接近最先进水平的AI推理能力，实现通信与计算的一体化。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [179] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT），刻画循环神经网络在学习过程中从混沌到稳定动态的转变，并预测网络输出随训练时间的演化，与数值模拟定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习会从根本上重塑循环神经网络的底层动力学，使其从初始混沌活动转变为稳定的任务依赖行为，但这一转变过程缺乏理论描述。

Method: 发展非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT）；将缓慢反馈驱动的学习过程建模为一个不断演化的有效反馈强度，该强度将网络推过 DMFT 解的分岔点，实现从混沌到稳定动态的转变；通过推导整个学习过程中的双时关联函数，确定临界反馈强度及依赖学习率的临界时间。

Result: 学习过程中网络的转变源于不断增长的反馈结构对有效动力学景观的渐进变形；理论预测了从初始未训练状态出发、训练期间网络输出的时间演化，并与数值模拟定量一致。

Conclusion: 非平衡 DMFT 成功揭示了循环神经网络在学习驱动下由混沌到稳定的动力学转变机制，为理解学习如何重塑网络动力学提供了可预测的理论框架。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [180] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化联邦分层高斯过程（pFedHGP），用于数据分散在异构客户端场景下的概率回归与分类。每个客户端的潜在函数分解为共享全局分量、客户端特定偏差和灵活局部残差三部分，并利用稀疏诱导变量近似与联邦变分推断，在保持原始数据本地的同时仅同步低维统计量，最终提供完整预测分布以支持不确定性决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习场景中，客户端数据通常具有异构性且受隐私约束，原始数据无法集中；而传统方法难以同时刻画全局共享结构、客户端个性化特征与局部灵活性，也缺乏完整的概率预测能力来支撑不确定性感知决策。

Method: pFedHGP 将每个客户端的潜在函数建模为三层结构：共享全局分量、共享全局核结构的客户端特定偏差、以及灵活的局部残差。模型采用稀疏诱导变量（inducing-variable）近似降低复杂度，并通过联邦变分推断（federated variational inference）进行训练，使原始数据保留在本地，服务器只同步共享分量的低维统计量。最终输出完整预测分布。

Result: 在应用研究中，pFedHGP 在压力吨位监测任务中仅使用 13.77% 的标注周期即实现完美的故障分类；在联邦空气质量建模中，无需集中各站点的时间序列即能恢复地理分区。此外，瞬时线性混合模型（Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model）视角将该层次结构与多输出高斯过程联系起来，用于相关传感器建模。

Conclusion: pFedHGP 通过联合建模全局共享与客户端个性化结构，在保护数据隐私的前提下实现了高效且具有不确定性量化能力的概率预测，并在故障分类和空气质量建模等实际任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [181] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“探针引导”(probe guidance)，利用现有扩散模型冻结的内部状态构造引导信号，无需推理时额外的前向传播，在连续扩散语言模型上实现无条件生成的新SOTA，并揭示了autoguidance的作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有autoguidance方法需要弱/强模型配对并在推理时进行额外前向传播，且要求两模型共享相似动态以保证引导有效，但其实际机制尚不清楚。作者旨在提供一种更高效、可靠的引导方法，并厘清autoguidance背后的原理。

Method: 提出probe guidance：直接使用现有扩散模型的冻结内部状态来构造引导信号，采用与autoguidance相似的原理，但省去推理时的额外前向传播，并天然保证弱、强模型共享相似的动力学。方法在连续扩散语言模型上应用并基准测试，同时利用探针研究传统autoguidance设置中强模型为弱checkpoint的情形。

Result: probe guidance在连续扩散语言模型的无条件生成上取得新的最先进性能；应用于1.7B扩散语言模型时，在多项选择题问答基准上持续提升表现。此外发现，传统autoguidance设置下弱模型必须来自训练的低熵区域才能有效。

Conclusion: probe guidance为改进扩散语言模型提供了实用方法，同时揭示弱模型需来自低熵训练区域这一条件，增进了对autoguidance机制的理解。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [182] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无线智能鞋垫平台与机器学习工作流，融合16点足底压力和六维IMU信号，对坐、站、行走和不稳定行走四种状态进行识别；在参与者独立的交叉验证下，直方图梯度提升（HGB）取得双足0.980的宏F1分数，且紧凑1D-CNN未显著优于HGB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒前常伴随移动能力、平衡和姿势转换的异常变化，因此需要一种低侵入、可穿戴的低剖面足部传感方案，在训练未见过的参与者上可靠推断活动状态，从而为老年照护中的活动监测与跌倒预防奠定基础。

Method: 开发无线智能鞋垫，每只鞋垫集成16个有源压力传感点和六维IMU（三轴加速度+三轴角速度）；15名健康成年人以80 Hz采样，信号分段为重叠窗口。先以分层10折交叉验证筛选窗口长度与候选模型族，再以参与者独立的5折Stratified Group交叉验证（同一参与者所有窗口留在同一折）获得主要性能估计。最终采用直方图梯度提升（HGB）模型，并与同折划分下的紧凑型1D-CNN对比（统计检验p=0.0625）。

Result: 在参与者独立评估下，HGB对左右脚分别取得0.954和0.959的宏F1，双足联合感知达到0.980；紧凑1D-CNN未显著优于HGB（p=0.0625）。结果表明低剖面足部传感可利用压力和IMU测量，为训练中未见过的参与者推断活动状态。

Conclusion: 该系统证明低剖面智能鞋垫融合足底压力与IMU信号能有效识别老年照护相关活动状态，具备泛化到新参与者的能力，为活动监测与跌倒预防提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [183] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在注意力机制的 query/key 投影矩阵上施加 Stiefel 流形约束，并用带信任域与极式收缩的黎曼 Adam 优化，证明其具有最速下降、尺度无关、良态及 O(d)-等变性，并通过权重衰减在 Stiefel 流形上梯度恒为零的机制加以解释，实验在 grokking 与 CIFAR-10 上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力中的 query/key 投影矩阵几乎总是由无几何约束的欧氏优化器训练。作者认为对这些矩阵施加 Stiefel 流形几何约束能带来更好的优化性质与更稳定的注意力几何，从而提升训练效果。

Method: 将 WQ、WK 约束到 Stiefel 流形 St(d,r)，在其上用黎曼 Adam 优化：每个 frame 携带一个标量二阶矩，通过信任域约束步长，并用极式（polar）收缩作 retraction。文中用五个命题证明该更新在嵌入度量下是最速下降、与梯度尺度无关、数值良态、且精确 O(d)-等变，并逐一在 float64 下数值验证。此外证明权重衰减在 St(d,r) 上的黎曼梯度恒为零（因 W = W·I_r 位于法空间），并做了消融（尺度自由、投影器、等变性）与规范移除的负结果分析。

Result: 在模算术 grokking 任务上，单次运行在 epoch 20000 达到 97.0% 验证准确率，对比基线 61.1%（作者强调该终点不稳定，仅作为机制证据而非效应量）。在 CIFAR-10 patches 上，该规则在 12 次配对起始中平均提升 +8.98 pp（t=60.6，12/12 全胜），且随数据增加差距扩大而非缩小。固定步长黎曼更新对梯度是一次（degree one）的，故每步移动量仅为同形 AdamW 矩阵的 1/24–1/40，frame 几乎不离初始化，冻结它们仅损失 0.28 pp。消融显示全部增益来自步长尺度自由，投影器与等变性无可测贡献。

Conclusion: 将 query/key 投影约束到 Stiefel 流形并采用尺度自由的黎曼更新，能在 grokking 与 CIFAR-10 上显著稳定并提升注意力几何的学习；增益机制在于步长的尺度自由，而非投影或等变性本身；规范移除无法作为该方法的动机，因为使损失不变的方向本身不携带梯度。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [184] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用锐度感知最小化（SAM）优化器提升拉曼光谱在临床细菌分类任务中的泛化能力，无需复杂预处理，相比传统 Adam 优化器最高提升 10.5% 的单次划分准确率，平均提升 2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌素耐药性预计到 2050 年每年将导致 1000 万人死亡，资源有限地区受影响最大。拉曼光谱是一种新型病原体诊断方法，可在数小时内完成便携式抗生素耐药性检测，远快于金标准方法的数天时间。然而现有拉曼光谱分析算法存在两大局限：1) 在跨不同患者群体的有限数据集上泛化能力差；2) 因拉曼光谱数据质量低而需要特征提取等非平凡预处理步骤，导致模型复杂度增加。本文旨在通过 SAM 优化器解决这些局限。

Method: 采用锐度感知最小化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization, SAM）优化器来增强模型在临床细菌分离株分类任务中跨多种超参数配置的泛化能力，并与传统 Adam 优化器在光谱分类任务上进行对比实验。

Result: SAM 在单次数据划分上相比 Adam 优化器实现最高 10.5% 的准确率提升；在所有数据划分的光谱分类任务中，平均准确率提升 2.7%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明 SAM 能够有效提升模型泛化能力，展现了其推动人工智能驱动的拉曼光谱工具在临床应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods. However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [185] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对小型临床表格数据，提出一种防泄漏的阈值似然朴素贝叶斯分类器，在PARI-HD队列中识别出I-309/CCL1是认知障碍可解释预测的最强增量特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型临床表格数据中深度学习常不可行、集成模型难以解释，而统计显著并不等于具有预测效用，因此需要一种既能保证防泄漏、又可解释且能严格评估特征增量预测价值的方法。

Method: 使用PARI-HD队列(n=165)，实现防泄漏的阈值似然伯努利/类别朴素贝叶斯(BNB/CNB)分类器。在每个训练折内，连续预测变量被归约为监督式卡方导出的状态，收入以类别似然进入模型。所有数据依赖步骤均在重复30次的10折分层交叉验证内完成，并以DeLong检验、200次随机划分、Benjamini-Hochberg校正评估特征增量价值。

Result: 人口学基线ROC-AUC为0.630±0.017。I-309(CCL1)是主导增量特征，AUC提升0.110，配对DeLong检验在100%的重复中p<0.05；预先指定的主分析得到固定划分DeLong p=0.0018，200次随机划分的中位p值为0.0011。在18个候选标志物探索族中，I-309在冻结划分上获得BH校正q=0.032，且在85%的随机划分中满足q<0.05，其余标志物均未显示可靠的增量预测价值。

Conclusion: 由于拟合模型是可检查的阈值和类条件概率表，结果将I-309/CCL1确定为认知障碍表格预测的可解释候选特征，但仍需外部验证加以确认。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [186] [Compressed Active Subspaces for Scalable Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19539)
*Thomas Flynn,Sanket Jantre,Byung-Jun Yoon,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出压缩活动子空间（CAS），通过结构化等距嵌入先将参数映射到压缩空间再构建活动子空间，大幅降低存储需求，使大规模模型上的贝叶斯推断成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准活动子空间方法在高维模型中量化预测不确定性时，需要存储大量全维模型梯度，随着模型规模增大，存储开销变得不可承受，限制了其可扩展性。

Method: 提出压缩活动子空间（CAS）：先用结构化等距嵌入将模型参数映射到低维压缩空间，再在该降维参数化内构建活动子空间，从而减少活动子空间构建所需的内存。

Result: CAS大幅降低了活动子空间构建的内存需求，使标准方法无法处理的大规模模型的贝叶斯推断成为可能，并在规模递增的神经网络上验证了可扩展性，同时保持了预测性能和稳健的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: CAS是一种可扩展的活动子空间方法，能有效降低内存开销并支持大规模模型的贝叶斯推断，在不牺牲预测性能和不确定性估计质量的前提下实现了可扩展性。

Abstract: Active subspace methods provide a framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty in high-dimensional models by identifying and performing inference along parameter directions that have the greatest influence on the model output. However, the construction of active subspaces requires storing many full-dimensional model gradients, which becomes prohibitive as model size increases. We address this limitation by proposing Compressed Active Subspaces (CAS), a scalable approach that first maps the model parameters to a compressed space using a structured isometric embedding and then constructs the active subspace within this reduced parameterization. Our approach substantially reduces the memory required for active subspace construction and enables Bayesian inference for large models where standard active subspace methods become impractical. We demonstrate the scalability of CAS on neural networks of increasing size while maintaining predictive performance and robust uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [187] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可行性反事实解释方法 FCx，基于改进的变分自编码器与多因子损失函数，能同时生成现实、低成本且可行的反事实解释，并支持硬约束与软约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成现实且低成本的反事实，但常忽视可行性，会提出不可构造的修改或与未来变化不兼容的建议（例如通过改变种族来获得工作机会）。因此需要一种显式强制可行性的反事实解释方法。

Method: 提出 FCx（Feasible Counterfactual Explanations），基于改进的变分自编码器（VAE）并以多因子损失函数优化。该方法融合两类约束：硬可行性约束（由领域知识用户指定）与软可行性约束（通过数据集上的因果推断自动推断）。成本由接近度（数值绝对变化）与稀疏性（被改变特征的数量）衡量，现实性则基于局部离群因子（LOF）进行密度估计，保证反事实位于高密度区域。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，FCx 在多个指标上达到最先进性能，同时保证可行性。

Conclusion: FCx 首次高效地生成同时满足现实性、低成本与可行性的反事实解释，通过显式约束与因果推断克服了以往方法的可行性缺陷，并在实验中验证了其有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [188] [Learn Your Own Thoughts: Abstract Token Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19717)
*Khashayar Gatmiry,Avrajit Ghosh,Parsa Mirtaheri,Jason D. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Emmanuel Abbe,Peter Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象令牌课程（ATC），一种无需显式思维令牌监督的课程学习框架，通过在连续表示空间中逐步增加问题复杂度，诱导模型形成内部抽象"思维"表示，并在理论上（奇偶函数学习）和实验上（图可达性与算术任务）验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链（CoT）技术虽然提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但需要对思维令牌进行显式监督，因而依赖大量任务特定的数据。作者希望在不依赖直接监督或手工设计scratchpad的情况下，激发有效的连续中间表示，从而降低数据要求并实现隐式推理。

Method: 提出抽象令牌课程（ATC）框架：通过一系列分布逐步提高问题复杂度进行课程式训练，使模型在连续表示空间中自发形成内部抽象"思维"表示。理论上分析了单层softmax注意力学习奇偶函数时，注意力会自然聚焦于上下文中提供"最易路径"预测下一令牌的CoT令牌。

Result: 理论上证明了ATC在奇偶函数学习场景下注意力机制会自然聚焦于关键CoT令牌；实验上，ATC在图可达性和算术学习任务上展现出有效性，并优于以往训练连续思维的方法。

Conclusion: ATC能够在不依赖显式思维令牌监督的情况下，通过课程学习在连续表示空间诱导出有效的抽象思维表示，为隐式推理训练提供了理论与实验支撑，并优于已有连续思维训练方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable reasoning capabilities by utilizing chain-of-thought (CoT) as a scratchpad for intermediate stages of thinking. However, CoT techniques require explicit supervision on thinking tokens, which requires rich, task-specific data. In this work, we propose Abstract Token Curriculum (ATC), a novel curriculum learning framework that elicits effective continuous intermediate representations without direct supervision or manual scratchpad design. ATC gradually increases problem complexity through a sequence of distributions, training the model to develop internal abstract ``thoughts'' in the continuous representation space. This paper provides both theoretical and experimental evidence for the benefits of ATC and its advantages over previous methods for training continuous thoughts. Theoretically, we show that for learning parity functions with single-layer softmax attention using ATC, attention naturally focuses on the CoT tokens in the context that provide the ``easiest path'' to predicting the next token. Experimentally, we show ATC's effectiveness on graph reachability and arithmetic learning tasks.

</details>


### [189] [Improving Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning via Selective Reward Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19414)
*Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSIQL（Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning），一种非层次化的离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过在离线轨迹中识别有助于目标推进的中间状态并施加额外奖励信号，以缓解稀疏奖励与长程依赖带来的延迟监督问题，性能媲美层次化方法但保持扁平策略结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习在稀疏奖励、长程依赖下十分困难：目标完成信息在时间上远离早期关键决策，同时离线价值估计引入额外误差。作者从奖励传播角度分析，在延迟目标设定下，目标导向的价值分离可能相对局部估计误差变得很小，从而难以有效学习。

Method: 提出 RSIQL，一种简单的非层次化方法：(1) 使用辅助的目标条件价值函数识别离线轨迹中估计能向目标取得进展的中间状态；(2) 对这些中间状态施加"奖励刺激"（reward stimulation），提供延迟更短的训练监督信号。与层次化方法不同，RSIQL 不学习独立的高层子目标策略，保持扁平结构。

Result: 在 D4RL 目标到达基准和 OGBench 上实验表明，RSIQL 平均表现优于目标条件 IQL，并达到与层次化离线目标条件方法相当的性能，同时保留了简单的扁平策略结构。

Conclusion: 通过在中间进展状态施加奖励刺激来缩短监督延迟，RSIQL 在无需层次化子目标策略的情况下，有效缓解了离线稀疏奖励与长程依赖问题，兼顾了性能与实现简洁性。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning aims to learn policies that reach specified goals, but remains challenging in offline settings with sparse rewards and long-horizon dependencies. In such settings, goal-completion information can be temporally distant from the early decisions that enable success, while offline value estimation introduces additional error. We study this issue from a reward-propagation perspective and show, in a stylized delayed-goal setting, how goal-directed value separation can become small relative to local estimation error. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL), a simple non-hierarchical method that introduces additional reward signals at progress-making intermediate states in offline trajectories. RSIQL uses an auxiliary goal-conditioned value function to identify intermediate states estimated to make progress toward the goal and applies reward stimulation to provide less-delayed training supervision. Unlike hierarchical methods, RSIQL does not learn a separate high-level subgoal policy. Experiments on D4RL goal-reaching benchmarks and OGBench show that RSIQL improves over goal-conditioned IQL on average and achieves performance competitive with hierarchical offline goal-conditioned methods, while retaining a simple flat policy structure.

</details>


### [190] [Bayesian Optimization with Rich Auxiliary Information via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19437)
*Tejus Gupta,Efe Mert Karagözlü,Rohit Sonker,Barnabás Póczos,Jeff Schnieder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）将丰富的辅助信息（如训练曲线、专家笔记、图像、先验知识）融入贝叶斯优化（BO），开发了三种方法，在超参数优化与核聚变任务中均优于标准BO和已有LLM优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实优化问题中往往存在比单纯函数评估更丰富的信息（训练曲线、专家笔记、图像、最优解位置的先验知识），但传统贝叶斯优化仅利用函数评估结果，无法有效利用这些辅助信息。

Method: 提出三种利用LLM将辅助信息融入贝叶斯优化的方法，以引导优化过程。

Result: 在多个超参数优化基准和一个真实核聚变优化任务上，所提方法一致优于标准贝叶斯优化以及现有基于LLM的优化方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明LLM能够有效利用丰富的辅助信息来改进贝叶斯优化，证实了LLM在贝叶斯优化中利用辅助信息的有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely used for optimizing expensive black-box functions, yet many real-world optimization problems contain substantially richer information than function evaluations alone. Examples include training curves in hyperparameter optimization, expert notes and images in scientific experimentation, and prior knowledge about where optima may lie. We show that large language models (LLMs) can effectively leverage such rich auxiliary information to guide optimization. Motivated by these findings, we develop three methods for incorporating auxiliary information into BO using LLMs. Across hyperparameter optimization benchmarks and a real-world nuclear fusion optimization task, our methods consistently outperform both standard BO and existing LLM-based optimization approaches. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLMs for leveraging rich auxiliary information in BO.

</details>


### [191] [When Does Retrieval Help Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20193)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Elham Buxton,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 检索插件为深度预测模型带来的增益，实际上取决于窗口长度 S 与主导季节周期 L 之间的关系（工作点），而非插件本身机制；分层评估揭示了这一"制度"。简单重复上一周期等对照方法在周期性强基准上普遍超过六种骨干网络与最强插件，且增益随周期变化，预训练也无法摆脱该瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对检索插件的评估都报告一致增益，并将之归功于各自机制，但作者怀疑真正原因是标准评估协议从未改变的窗口长度与季节周期的关系，需要揭示这一真正的收益来源，避免错误归因。

Method: 通过按"窗口长度 S 与主导季节周期 L 的关系"对评估进行分层，提出一个对照协议（如重复上一周期的简单对照、无相位可恢复的配对对照、精确查表与图扩散对照）；并在合成数据上对 horizon、period、window 做受控扫描；用趋势检验和停滞率两个可解释统计量预测逐格收益符号；用 leave-one-dataset-out 评估验证其预测力。

Result: S=12 时，重复上一周期的简单对照在 7 个基准中的 4 个上按 MSE 超出六种骨干 8%-44%，超过最强插件（ETTm1）或持平（ECL）；在训练谱缺乏集中共享周期的 3 个数据集上反而差最多 25%。合成扫描显示收益边界与周期相关（+0.71），与 horizon 弱负相关（-0.23）。配对对照几乎消除效应，与相位缺乏一致。零样本预训练基础模型在周期基准上落后训练骨干 22%-50%；精确查表与图扩散效果相同。两个可解释统计量（趋势检验、停滞率）在留一数据集评估下以 0.76 准确率预测逐格收益符号，超过 0.69 多数规则，而 22 特征堆叠仅为 0.57。

Conclusion: 检索插件收益的真正来源是工作点（窗口与周期的关系）而非插件机制，存在可预测的"制度图"；作者不提出新插件，而是贡献制度图、揭示它的协议，以及部署前筛查收益的两个统计量，并公开代码。

Abstract: Retrieval plug-ins supply a deep forecaster with information its lookback window cannot carry. Published evaluations report consistent gains, and each credits its own mechanism. We show that the benefit belongs instead to the operating point: the relation between window length $S$ and dominant seasonal period $L$, an axis the standard protocol never varies. Stratifying the evaluation by that relation exposes the regime. At $S{=}12$, a simple control that repeats the last observed period beats the six standard backbones, in aggregate, on four of seven benchmarks by $8\%$ to $44\%$ of MSE. It beats the strongest plug-in we run on ETTm1 and matches it on ECL. It is worse by up to $25\%$ on the three datasets whose training-split spectra lack a concentrated, shared period. A controlled synthetic sweep of horizon, period, and window shows the benefit boundary tracks the period (correlation $+0.71$), not the horizon ($-0.23$). A paired control with no phase to recover nearly erases the effect, consistent with phase starvation. Zero-shot pretraining does not escape it: a foundation model trails trained backbones by $22\%$ to $50\%$ on the periodic benchmarks. Within our instrument, exact lookup matches graph diffusion: the payoff is consulting the record, not the machinery on top. Two interpretable statistics, a trend test and a staleness rate, predict the sign of the per-cell benefit at $0.76$ accuracy under leave-one-dataset-out evaluation, a suggestive margin over the $0.69$ majority rule, where a 22-feature stack manages $0.57$. We propose no new plug-in. The contribution is the regime map, the protocol that reveals it, and two statistics that screen it before deployment. Code: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/retrieval-regime.

</details>


### [192] [Enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network by Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19466)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Rose Chong-Wu,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出知识增强的农业信息神经网络（KAINN），通过将肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和充水孔隙度等领域知识融入混合神经-机理框架，并在 CNN、LSTM 和 Transformer 架构上验证，结果显示其预测农业 N2O 排放的误差更低、R² 更高，且学习到的参数轨迹更平滑、物理一致性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业 N2O 排放预测对评估环境影响和支持可持续农业至关重要，但由于排放受土壤性质、气候、生化过程和管理措施等复杂因素交互作用影响，且高质量观测数据有限，预测十分困难。纯深度学习模型虽能捕捉非线性关系，但缺乏物理可解释性，且跨环境条件下的泛化能力较差。

Method: 提出知识增强的农业信息神经网络（KAINN），一种混合神经-机理框架，在农业信息神经网络（AINN）基础上融入肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和充水孔隙度等领域知识；分别采用 CNN、LSTM 和 Transformer 三种架构，并在多个生长季节和多种输入特征配置下进行评估。

Result: 与纯数据驱动模型和原始 AINN 相比，KAINN 普遍具有更低的 RMSE 与 MAE 以及更高的 R² 值；对学习到的接口分析表明，其参数轨迹更平滑、物理一致性更强，且不确定性降低。

Conclusion: 将环境知识融入神经网络可提升农业 N2O 排放预测的可靠性、可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from agriculture is important for assessing environmental impacts and supporting sustainable farming. However, prediction remains difficult because N2O emissions result from complex interactions among soil properties, climate, biochemical processes, and management practices, while high-quality observations are limited. Deep learning models can capture nonlinear relationships but often lack physical interpretability and may generalize poorly across environmental conditions. We propose the Knowledge-enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network (KAINN), a hybrid neural-mechanistic framework that extends the Agriculture-informed Neural Network by incorporating domain knowledge about fertilizer diffusion, soil respiration, and water-filled porosity. We evaluate KAINN using CNN, LSTM, and Transformer architectures across multiple growing seasons and input-feature configurations. The results show that KAINN generally provides lower root mean square error and mean absolute error and higher R-squared values than purely data-driven models and the original AINN. Analysis of the learned interfaces also shows smoother and more physically consistent parameter trajectories with reduced uncertainty. These findings demonstrate that incorporating environmental knowledge into neural networks can improve the reliability, interpretability, and generalization of agricultural N2O-emission predictions.

</details>


### [193] [The Bias of Nonlinear Two-Time-scale Stochastic Approximation under Constant Step-Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20409)
*Djamel Rassem Lamouri,Dorian Baudry,Nicolas Gast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性双时间尺度随机逼近（TTSA）在常数步长 α≫β 下的有限时间收敛性，给出均方误差与偏差的紧界 O(α+β²/α²)，并揭示其与线性情形的定性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时间尺度随机逼近是分析强化学习、优化与随机控制中耦合迭代算法的基本工具，但非线性双时间尺度方案在常数步长下的有限时间保证一直难以获得，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在标准的稳定性、正则性与马尔可夫噪声假设下，对非线性 TTSA（步长 α≫β）进行分析。将初始条件、快时间尺度跟踪误差、马尔可夫依赖性与时间尺度耦合等各因素的贡献分离，从而上界两迭代点围绕其极限平衡点的均方误差与偏差。

Result: 得到均方误差与偏差的上界 O(α+β²/α²)，并证明当 β≤α^(3/2) 时该界是紧的。分析厘清了 β²/α² 项的来源，并揭示非线性动力学引入了线性 TTSA 中所没有的额外有限时间效应。

Conclusion: 非线性 TTSA 的有限时间行为与线性情形存在定性差异，非线性动力学带来的额外效应使得误差界呈现 α+β²/α² 的形式，且该界在 β≤α^(3/2) 范围内是紧的。

Abstract: Two-timescale stochastic approximation (TTSA) is a fundamental tool for analyzing coupled iterative algorithms in reinforcement learning, optimization, and stochastic control. However, finite-time guarantees for nonlinear two-timescale schemes remain difficult to obtain, especially under constant step-sizes. In this paper, we study nonlinear TTSA with step-sizes $α\ggβ$. Under standard stability, regularity, and Markovian noise assumptions, we upper bound the mean-squared error and the bias of both iterates around their limiting equilibria. Our bounds scale as $O(α+β^2/α^2)$, which we prove to be tight when $β\leα^{3/2}$. The analysis separates the contributions of initial conditions, fast-timescale tracking error, Markovian dependence, and timescale coupling, thereby clarifying the origin of the $β^2/α^2$ term. Our results reveal qualitative differences from the linear TTSA setting previously studied, showing that nonlinear dynamics introduce additional finite-time effects that are absent in the linear case.

</details>


### [194] [Search at the Cost of Sampling: Nearly-Instant Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19476)
*Donney Fan,Colin Doumont,Aleksandra Kalisz,Paul Duckworth,Jacob R. Gardner,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对去新生设计流水线中贝叶斯优化(BO)计算开销过大的问题，提出利用线性代理模型与高维潜变量集中分布的球面域特性，推导出近闭式解的代理建模与采集函数算法，实现了比现有最优基线至少100倍的速度提升，使BO成为可实际部署的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在去新生设计流水线中日益重要，但传统BO因其顺序决策的计算开销过高，在虚拟筛选成本较低的场景下成为瓶颈，导致BO虽理论契合却无法实用。

Method: 基于近期关于线性代理模型合理性的研究，利用线性模型约束于球面域这一特性（高维潜变量在该区域集中分布），利用球对称性推导出代理建模与采集函数问题的近闭式解。

Result: 相比当前最优基线方法实现了至少100倍的加速，同时在分子生成与图像生成基准上取得匹配或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法将BO从"太慢而无法考虑"转变为去新生流水线中可即插即用的实用工具。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly central to many de novo discovery pipelines, in which designs are generated at scale and filtered through virtual screens to determine a set of candidates to experimentally validate. While Bayesian optimization (BO) is a natural fit for this setting, as it uses past evaluations to guide future proposals, the computational overhead required for its sequential decision-making becomes a bottleneck when virtual screens are relatively cheap. We make BO practical in this regime by exploiting the unique combination of a linear model constrained to a spherical domain where high-dimensional latents concentrate. We build off recent work justifying the use of linear surrogates, while deriving nearly closed-form solutions to the surrogate modelling and acquisition problems that exploit spherical symmetry. The result is at least a 100x speedup over state-of-the art baselines, with matching or improved performance across molecular and image generation benchmarks. Altogether, our method makes BO a practical drop-in for de novo pipelines where it was previously too slow to consider.

</details>


### [195] [Parallelism, critical windows, and separations among diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20539)
*Sitan Chen,Liye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文首次对三种主流扩散大语言模型（masked、uniform、Gaussian diffusion）的并行生成能力进行细粒度比较,证明 uniform 与 Gaussian 扩散能以远小于上下文长度的前向传播次数采样,并首次建立了三者之间的可证并行性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM 以并行性为核心卖点,但不同扩散范式(masked/uniform/Gaussian)在并行能力上的原理性比较仍然缺乏,现有结果仅对 masked diffusion 有明确复杂度刻画,亟需统一的理论框架来理解三种方法的并行性差异。

Method: 作者借助总对偶相关(dual total correlation)这一内在复杂度度量,刻画 uniform 与 Gaussian 扩散采样所需的前向传播次数;并针对一族随机经验测度,通过构造匹配的上下界(necessity and sufficiency)证明前向传播次数上界 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{d})$,同时构造近似分数预言机(oracle)证明 masked diffusion 需 $\widetilde{\Omega}(d)$ 次前向传播,从而建立可证分离。

Result: 证明 uniform 和 Gaussian 扩散可在前向传播次数随总对偶相关缩放(可远小于上下文长度)的条件下采样,此前的已知结果仅针对 masked diffusion;并在一族随机经验测度上证明 uniform/Gaussian 扩散 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{d})$ 次前向传播即必要且充分,而 masked diffusion 在近似分数预言机下需要 $\widetilde{\Omega}(d)$ 次,建立了三种主流 dLLM 范式在并行性上的首个可证分离。

Conclusion: 与"masked diffusion 因必须确定 token 值而更难并行"的流行直觉相反,该分离的真正原因在于 masked diffusion 采样中的临界窗口(critical windows)渐近上比 uniform 和 Gaussian 扩散更窄。

Abstract: A popular selling point of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is their capacity for parallelism: the ability to generate sequences of text far more efficiently than autoregressive models, which require one forward pass per token. Yet among the many competing paradigms for dLLMs, from masked to uniform to Gaussian diffusion, principled understanding of how these different proposals compare in parallelism remains limited. In this work, we initiate a fine-grained comparison of the capacity for parallelism among these three leading approaches and prove the following:
  - Uniform and Gaussian diffusion can sample in a number of forward passes which scales with the dual total correlation of the underlying distribution, a measure of intrinsic complexity which can be much smaller than the context length. Previously, it was only known how to achieve this using masked diffusion.
  - For a certain family of random empirical measures, we show that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{d})$ forward passes are necessary and sufficient to sample using uniform or Gaussian diffusion, yet there exist approximate score oracles for which $\widetildeΩ(d)$ forward passes are needed for masked diffusion. This establishes the first provable separation in parallelism between the three prevailing dLLM paradigms.
  Contrary to popular intuition that masked diffusions are harder to parallelize because they must commit to token values, the latter separation instead comes from the fact that the critical windows in masked diffusion sampling are asymptotically narrower than those in uniform and Gaussian diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [196] [Sample Count Is Not Enough: Candidate-Generation Strategy Shapes the Energy and Performance of LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19499)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出采样式测试时扩展中，候选数 N 不足以刻画系统成本；相同候选数在不同生成调度（如 1x8 对比 8x1）下，能耗与延迟差异巨大，批处理更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展研究通常用候选数 N 描述推理预算，但 N 只说明生成了多少候选，未说明它们如何被调度执行。作者希望揭示这种“调度维度”在系统成本上的显著差异，推动评估更全面地报告系统指标。

Method: 在 GSM8K 500 条提示上，用 Phi-3-mini 和 Qwen2.5-1.5B 研究 N 对推理准确率的影响（N 从 1 到 8）；随后固定 N=8，对比四种生成调度（1x8、2x4、4x2、8x1，axb 表示 a 次生成调用、每次 b 个候选），在 A100 GPU 上测量延迟、吞吐、GPU-小时和 GPU 设备总能耗，并在 SciQ/V100 短输出任务及三台独立调度的 A100 节点上复现验证。

Result: 增加 N 从 1 到 8 使 Phi-3-mini 准确率提升 8.4 个百分点、Qwen2.5-1.5B 提升 18.4 个百分点。固定 N=8 时，8 次串行调用的 GPU 设备总能耗为 1 次批量调用的 4.64–4.86 倍，P95 延迟为 5.77–6.12 倍；该规律在三个独立调度节点及 SciQ/V100 实验中均成立。

Conclusion: 仅用候选数不足以描述多候选测试时扩展的系统成本；当候选相互独立且显存允许时，更少次数、更大批量大小的生成调用更高效。评估应同时报告候选数、准确率、生成调度以及 GPU 级系统指标。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can improve large language model reasoning by generating and combining multiple candidate responses. In sampling-based methods, the inference budget is often described by the number of generated candidates, N. However, N tells us how many candidates are generated, not how they are executed. The same candidate budget can be produced in one batched generation call or split across several sequential calls with smaller batch sizes. We first study the effect of increasing N on reasoning accuracy using Phi-3-mini and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 500 GSM8K prompts. As expected, increasing N from 1 to 8 improves accuracy by 8.4 percentage points for Phi-3-mini and 18.4 points for Qwen2.5-1.5B. However, accuracy alone does not show the systems cost of using a larger candidate budget. We therefore fix N = 8 and compare four generation schedules: 1x8, 2x4, 4x2, and 8x1, where axb denotes a generation calls with b candidates per call. We measure latency, throughput, GPU-hours, and gross GPU-device energy while keeping the total candidate count fixed. On A100 GPUs, eight serial calls use 4.64-4.86x as much gross GPU-device energy and have 5.77-6.12x the P95 latency of one batched call with eight candidates. The same pattern appears across three independently scheduled A100 nodes per model and in short-output SciQ/V100 experiments. These results show that candidate count alone is not enough to describe the systems cost of multi-candidate test-time scaling. When candidates are independent and memory allows it, fewer generation calls with larger batch sizes are more efficient. Evaluations should therefore report not only candidate count and accuracy, but also generation schedule and GPU-level systems metrics.

</details>


### [197] [Epidemiological Causal Graph Identification: Challenges, Identifiability and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20676)
*Sambit Mishra,Yingying Wang,Christine K. Johnson,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合数据（序数变量与单参数指数族变量）在DAG中的因果发现，证明序数节点与指数族节点间边的方向在通用参数下是可分布的，并提出两种算法（基于得分的穷举搜索与基于DAGMA的掩码连续优化）进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现的可识别性研究主要关注加性噪声模型下的连续变量，忽略了包含序数、计数和连续测量的混合数据集，而这类数据在实践中广泛存在且方向识别缺乏理论保证。

Method: 在DAG框架下，节点服从有序logit模型所刻画的有序分布或规则的单参数指数族分布；从分布可识别性角度证明序数节点与指数族节点之间边方向的确定条件；计算上提出基于得分的穷举搜索，以及利用DAGMA的掩码连续优化框架处理更大规模图。

Result: 证明了序数节点与指数族节点之间边的方向在通用参数值下具有分布可识别性，将已有的Ordinal-Poisson结果推广到更广泛的指数族；数值实验成功恢复了马尔可夫等价类中在经典结构方程模型下不可识别的边方向。

Conclusion: 本文为混合（序数与指数族）数据上DAG的因果方向识别提供了可识别性理论与实用算法，扩展了因果发现对混合数据类型的适用性，并通过数值实验验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is fundamental to statistics and machine learning, yet determining causal direction without interventions necessitates structural assumptions. Existing identifiability research primarily focuses on continuous variables under additive noise models, often neglecting mixed datasets containing ordinal scales, counts, and continuous measurements. This paper investigates causal discovery in Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) where nodes follow either an ordinal distribution (via an ordered logit model) or a regular one-parameter exponential family distribution. We prove that the edge direction between an ordinal and an exponential family node is distributionally identifiable for generic parameter values. Our findings generalize previous Ordinal-Poisson results to the broader exponential family. Computationally, we introduce a score-based exhaustive search and a masked continuous optimization framework using DAGMA for larger graphs. Numerical results validate the theory, recovering edge orientations within a Markov equivalence class that are unidentifiable under classical structural equation models.

</details>


### [198] [LSTM-UT and Recurrent-Depth Transformers on Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19521)
*Aras Kavuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较了三种递归深度 Transformer（BUT、CoTFormer、LSTM-UT）在信息保留方式上的差异，结果显示加入有界门控记忆的 LSTM-UT 在深度外推和延迟回忆两项任务上均优于前两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归深度 Transformer 都通过重复共享计算来模拟深度推理，但它们在各计算步骤之间保留信息的方式不同，而这些差异如何影响模型的泛化能力（尤其是深度外推和后续检索）尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在规则 30 元胞自动机任务上比较三种模型：仅保留当前隐藏状态的 Block Universal Transformer（BUT）、额外保留不断增长的注意力缓存的 CoTFormer、以及新提出的带界门控记忆的 LSTM Universal Transformer（LSTM-UT）。此外，通过状态干预和缓存干预实验分析 CoTFormer 失效的机制，并在延迟回忆任务中测试模型对历史表示的检索能力。

Result: 在规则 30 任务上，BUT 对未见递归深度的外推比 CoTFormer 更可靠，尽管其准确率最终也会下降；干预实验表明 CoTFormer 的失效源于当前状态与保留历史的交互——纠正当前状态可暂时恢复准确率，而保留的历史会削弱这种纠正。在延迟回忆任务中，BUT 也优于 CoTFormer，因为 CoTFormer 无法可靠地选出所请求的缓存表示。LSTM-UT 在深度外推和延迟回忆两项任务上均优于这些基线。

Conclusion: 有界门控记忆是一个有效的归纳偏置，能够同时改善这些任务中的重复计算和后续检索能力。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth Transformers apply shared computation repeatedly, but differ in how they retain information across steps. We compare a Block Universal Transformer (BUT), which carries only its current hidden state; CoTFormer, which also retains an expanding attention cache; and a new LSTM Universal Transformer (LSTM-UT) with bounded gated memory. On Rule 30 cellular automata, BUT extrapolates to unseen recurrent depths more reliably than CoTFormer, although its accuracy eventually degrades. State and cache interventions show that CoTFormer's failure depends on their interaction: correcting the current state can temporarily restore accuracy, while retained history can undermine that correction. In a delayed-recall task, BUT also outperforms CoTFormer despite lacking direct access to past states; CoTFormer does not reliably select the requested cached representation. LSTM-UT improves both depth extrapolation and delayed recall over these baselines. The results support bounded gated memory as an effective inductive bias for repeated computation and later retrieval in these tasks.

</details>


### [199] [FedFIbOS: Fisher Importance based Optimal Submodelling for Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19559)
*Yasmeen Afzal,Jeremiah D. Deng,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedFIbOS，一种基于 Fisher 信息重要性度量的异构联邦学习子模型选择方法，通过从最小化子模型掩码误差推导出的原则性准则，替代缺乏理论依据的启发式幅度选择，并在收敛框架下证明了原始 Fisher top-k 规则在 Fisher 主导排序条件下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦学习中各客户端计算能力不同，需训练容量受限的子模型。现有方法使用启发式重要性度量（尤其是参数幅度）来选择子模型参数，但缺乏理论依据说明这些度量为何能保证收敛；同时部分客户端参与会引入 Fisher 分数的额外估计效应。作者识别出这一根本性缺口：现有参数选择准则缺乏收敛框架下的理论支撑。

Method: 提出 FedFIbOS（Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling）。以 Fisher 信息为核心，从最小化子模型掩码误差出发推导出原则性选择准则；通过 Fisher 加权的二次掩码代理（surrogate）形式化子模型选择问题，并证明在 Fisher 主导排序条件下，FedFIbOS 实现的原始 Fisher top-k 规则可求解该代理问题，从而保留底层掩码联邦优化界的收敛结构。Fisher 分数利用平方梯度从经验对角 Fisher 信息中高效估计，无需额外优化开销，可实现稳定且自适应的参数选择。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 和 AGNews 上的病态（pathological）与 Dirichlet 非 IID 设置下，FedFIbOS 比当前最优方法（state of the art）精度高出约 10%，且异构性越强，提升越明显。

Conclusion: FedFIbOS 为异构联邦学习中的子模型参数选择提供了有理论保证的 Fisher 信息准则，弥补了启发式幅度选择缺乏收敛理论支撑的空白；其高效估计方式不增加优化开销，并在多种非 IID 场景下显著优于现有最优方法，尤其在强异构条件下表现更突出。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning requires clients with diverse computational capacities to collaboratively train a global model, where each client trains a capacity-constrained submodel. Existing methods select submodel parameters using heuristic importance measures---most prominently parameter magnitude---without theoretical justification for why these measures support convergence. We identify a fundamental gap: existing parameter selection criteria lack theoretical grounding in the convergence framework, partial client participation introduces additional estimation effects in the Fisher scores. We propose \textbf{FedFIbOS}: Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling for heterogeneous federated learning, using Fisher Information in a principled criterion derived from minimizing submodel masking error. %We formally establish when magnitude selection is equivalent to Fisher selection fail under non-IID heterogeneous federated learning. We theoretically formulate submodel selection through a Fisher-weighted quadratic masking surrogate and show that the raw Fisher top-$k$ rule implemented by FedFIbOS solves this surrogate under a Fisher-dominant ranking condition. The resulting method retains the convergence structure of the underlying masked federated optimization bound. Fisher scores are efficiently estimated from empirical diagonal Fisher information using squared gradients, enabling stable and adaptive parameter selection without additional optimization overhead. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and AGNews under pathological and Dirichlet non-IID settings show FedFIbOS achieves ${\approx}10\%$ higher accuracy than the state of the art, with improvements becoming more pronounced under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [200] [The Complexity Kink: A Prompt-Side Structural Complexity Index for Code-Generation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19616)
*Michael Hernandez,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在生成前基于提示词（prompt）六维结构复杂度指数，将其与正确性解耦，并在21个模型、105,000次生成上验证该指数与通过率之间呈非单调"分段"关系，但该关系随任务类型、构造框架和具体模型而变化，因此仅作观测性测量框架而非因果断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从生成代码中测量的复杂度是"依赖结果"的：一个困难提示可能产出很短的失败程序，从而被错误地赋予低输出复杂度。作者希望构建一个在生成之前就能评分的、独立于正确性的提示侧结构复杂度指标，以更可靠地刻画提示难度及其与模型通过率的关系。

Method: 作者构建六维提示侧结构复杂度指数，在初步单一评分者量表的六个分段中选择5,000个Python提示；由四位面板外LLM评分者复评锁定提示，得到19,997条评分行（4,998个含全部四评的提示），复合评分者间信度ICC=0.872。对每个提示评估21个模型，共105,000次生成。采用均值池化、任务类型固定效应、构造框架控制、模型特定拟合、Lizard复杂度交叉分析、分歧富集校准集上的人类一致性评估、改写与跨语言复评，以及过度识别检验和2SLS等方法进行观测性分析。

Result: 未调整的均值池化分析中，通过率在复合分13.75处出现非单调断点：低于或等于该值通过率79.9%，高于该值87.6%（+7.6点）。加入任务类型固定效应后断点移至10.75，区间差从7.6缩小到2.1点；加入构造框架控制后断点移至8.50，原始差为-3.5点，且单一框架均无法复现池化的+7.6点变化。模型特定拟合中16个模型向上、5个向下变化。365个提示的审计清洁扩展在15与16分箱上与原始五模型估计吻合，但仅新增14个高于16分箱的提示。在可计算Lizard复杂度的零通过生成中，28.5%同时满足提示复合分>8且输出复杂度≤10。人类一致性在分歧富集校准集上中等且依赖评分者；改写与跨语言复评保持评分顺序。过度识别检验拒绝六维联合约束，故将复合分视为指数而不对2SLS估计作因果解释。

Conclusion: 该复合复杂度指数是一个可靠的预生成测量框架，可用于观测性描述通过率的"可靠性区间"；但由于断点随任务类型、构造框架和模型而变化，且过度识别检验拒绝联合约束，不能将其解释为通用的失败临界值或因果关系。

Abstract: Complexity measured from generated code is failure-dependent: a difficult prompt can yield a short failing program and be assigned low output complexity. We introduce a six-dimension prompt-side structural-complexity index scored before generation and kept separate from correctness. We select 5,000 Python prompts across six bands of a preliminary single-rater rubric. Four out-of-panel LLM raters rescore the locked prompts, giving 19,997 score rows; composite inter-rater reliability is ICC = 0.872 on the 4,998 prompts with all four ratings. We evaluate 21 models per prompt, yielding 105,000 generations. In the unadjusted mean-pooled analysis, pass rate has a nonmonotone breakpoint at composite 13.75, with 79.9% at or below and 87.6% above. This is not a universal failure cutoff. Task-type fixed effects shift the breakpoint to 10.75 and cut the regime gap from 7.6 to 2.1 points. A construction-frame control shifts it to 8.50 with a raw gap of -3.5 points, and neither frame alone reproduces the pooled +7.6-point change. Model-specific fits include 16 upward and five downward changes. A 365-prompt audit-clean extension matches the original five-model estimates at bins 15 and 16 but adds only 14 prompts above bin 16. Among zero-pass generations with computable Lizard complexity, 28.5% pair a prompt composite above 8 with output complexity at most 10. Human agreement is moderate and rater-dependent on a disagreement-enriched calibration set; paraphrase and cross-language rescoring preserve score ordering. Overidentification tests reject the joint restrictions on the six dimensions, so we treat the composite as an index and make no causal interpretation of the 2SLS estimates. The contribution is a pre-generation measurement framework and a bounded observational analysis of reliability regimes.

</details>


### [201] [A Policy Profile for Croissant: Refusal as a Property of the Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19640)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 Croissant（ML 数据集机器可读描述符）补充了其缺失的"评估语义"半边：通过一个叠加式 profile，为数据使用条件定义了一套封闭的五运算符决策过程，使门禁（gate）能仅凭描述符本身作出并记录决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croissant 自 1.1 版起虽推荐用 DUO 和 ODRL 表达数据使用条件，但从未规定任何评估语义：无决策过程、无评估成本界限、无法评估时无结果约定、无检查记录，也未涉及与调用方权限的组合。作者旨在补全这缺失的"半边"。

Method: 提出一个叠加式（additive）profile：数据集声明其允许的操作及其条件，基于一个封闭的五运算符集合，并完整给出决策过程，使门禁仅凭描述符即可作出决策并记录所检查的内容。用两个语料评估：其一来自真实 nf-core 流水线的三个描述符（评估部署效果）；其二由该 profile 的文法生成（评估广度，覆盖每个运算符、拒绝类与一致性条款）。

Result: 部署评估：profile 文档作出的决策与原门禁逐条一致，剥离该层后仍是合法的 Croissant 文档，新增成本为 11.7 μs（相对 119 μs 的决策时间）。广度评估：552 条完整决策记录在三种路径下（原生描述符、profile 术语、usageInfo 中同一策略的 ODRL 表达）三方一致。结论是载体（carrier）并非贡献所在，评估语义才是。调用方策略与数据方策略分布在互不重叠的状态空间上，故二者的允许集互不包含。

Conclusion: 本文提供了 Croissant 数据使用条件的评估语义（而非新的表达载体），以叠加式 profile 实现封闭的五运算符决策过程，通过部署与广度两类语料验证其正确性、兼容性与低成本，并厘清了调用方与数据方策略的独立性。

Abstract: Croissant is the de facto machine-readable descriptor for ML datasets: JSON-LD over schema.org. Since version 1.1 it also carries data use conditions, recommending DUO and ODRL for them. What no version specifies is how any of them is evaluated: no decision procedure, no bound on evaluation cost, no outcome for a condition an implementation cannot evaluate, no record of what was checked, and nothing on composition with caller-side authority. We supply that half. An additive profile lets a dataset declare the operations it admits and the conditions under which it admits them, over a closed set of five operators whose decision procedure is given in full, so a gate decides from the descriptor alone and records what it checked. Two corpora evaluate it and their evidence is kept apart. Three descriptors that gated a real nf-core pipeline give the deployment result: decisions from a profile document match the gate's native descriptor record for record, stripping the layer leaves a valid Croissant document, and the added cost is 11.7 $μ$s against a 119 $μ$s decision. A corpus generated from the profile's grammar gives the breadth, covering every operator, refusal class and conformance clause. Across its valid cases, 552 complete decision records agree three ways -- native descriptor, profile terms, and the same policy as ODRL in usageInfo. The carrier is therefore not the contribution; the evaluation semantics is. Finally, caller-bound and data-bound policies range over non-overlapping state spaces, so neither permit set contains the other.

</details>


### [202] [CoRe: Coherence and Relational Alignment for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19670)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Huiran Duan,Yining Liu,Zhixiang Wu,Chu Lin,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRe 是一个与模型无关的多元时间序列直接预测训练目标，用频率相干损失和低秩关系图损失替代逐点误差，在不引入额外可训练参数的情况下提升多种主干模型的预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接预测已成为多元时序预测的标准范式，但其训练目标仍常被分解为逐点误差（如 MSE）。这类目标监督稳定，但无法显式保持未来轨迹的结构：既削弱了每个变量内部的时间连贯性，也削弱了变量之间的关系一致性。
