Motivation: 联邦学习中数据生成分布可能在不同客户端间存在差异,且真实混合比例往往未知,导致底层群组分布难以准确刻画;现有方法虽通过对抗最坏情形混合处理跨客户端不确定性,却假设各客户端分布估计是精确的,而有限样本下的估计并不可靠,因此需要同时应对跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内分布模糊性。

Method: 将全局模糊集构造为各客户端局部模糊集的可容许混合之并,允许客户端特定的模糊半径,并得到按客户端可分离的重构形式;基于该结构建立高概率样本外性能保证;进一步针对罚函数重构给出联邦算法,并在较弱的正则性条件下证明收敛。

Result: 建立了一个高概率的样本外(泛化)性能保证,证明所提联邦算法在更温和的正则性条件下收敛,并通过数值仿真验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够同时处理跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内分布模糊性,具有理论上的样本外性能保证与收敛保证,数值实验结果支持其有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning trains a shared model from private client data. In practice, data-generating distributions may differ, and the true mixture across clients is often unknown, making the underlying group distribution difficult to specify. Existing approaches address cross-client mixture uncertainty by optimizing against the worst-case mixture, yet assume accurate client-wise distribution estimates. However, these estimates can be unreliable when based on finite samples. To handle both cross-client mixture uncertainty and within-client distributional ambiguity, we construct a global ambiguity set as the union of admissible mixtures of local ambiguity sets. The construction allows client-specific ambiguity radii and admits a client-wise separable reformulation. Leveraging this structure, we establish a high-probability out-of-sample performance guarantee. We further develop a federated algorithm for a penalty-based reformulation and prove its convergence under milder regularity conditions. Simulations validate the algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [269] [Radio Frequency Detection and Classification of Microplastics in Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20507)
*Jaden Tolbert,Md Saiful Islam,Pingshan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习辅助的射频介电光谱细胞计数（RF DiSC）平台，用于水中微塑料（MPs）的无标记检测与分类。通过对8种10 μm微塑料在0.2–9 GHz四个频点测量S参数变化并训练监督学习模型，在去离子水中实现了宏平均F1、精确率和召回率均超过0.71，且在含3.3%和6.6%海盐的盐水介质中PET分类性能基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微塑料/纳米塑料作为普遍存在的环境污染物，其丰度日益增加且具有潜在健康风险，急需快速、无标记的检测方法。传统光学和光谱技术在颗粒尺寸降至低微米量级时，因通量有限和/或样品制备复杂而变得困难。

Method: 开发机器学习辅助的射频介电光谱细胞计数（RF DiSC）平台：将8种标称直径10 μm的微塑料颗粒悬浮于去离子水中，在0.2–9 GHz的四个频率下测量射频散射参数（S参数）相对于载体介质的变化，并以此训练监督机器学习模型进行材料分类，同时包括混合样品及盐水环境中的微塑料识别。

Result: 对去离子水中悬浮的8类微塑料，方法达到的宏平均F1分数、精确率和召回率均超过0.71；在含3.3%和6.6%海盐的盐水载体介质中，PET的分类性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 结果证明了机器学习辅助的RF DiSC在含水环境中进行快速、单颗粒微塑料分类的可行性。未来工作将聚焦于通过改进射频校准、扩展光谱覆盖范围、扩大训练数据集，以及使用环境老化和生物污染的微塑料进行验证，以提升分类性能。

Abstract: Micro- and nano-plastic particles (MPs/NPs) are ubiquitous environmental contaminants whose increasing abundance and potential health impacts have created an urgent need for rapid, label-free detection methods. As particle size decreases to the low-micrometer range, conventional optical and spectroscopic techniques become increasingly challenging because of limited throughput and/or complex sample preparation. In this work, we present a machine learning (ML)-assisted radio-frequency (RF) dielectric spectroscopic cytometry (DiSC) platform for the label-free detection and classification of MPs. Eight types of $ 10 $ μm nominal-diameter MP particles suspended in deionized (DI) water were characterized at four frequencies spanning $ 0.2\text{-}9\text{ GHz} $. The measured alterations in RF scattering parameters (S-parameters), referenced to the carrier medium, were used to train supervised ML models for material classification, including the identification of MPs in mixed samples and saline-water environments. For eight MP classes suspended in DI water, the proposed method achieved macro-average F1-score, precision, and recall values exceeding $ 0.71 $. Furthermore, PET classification performance was largely maintained in saline carrier media containing $3.3\% $ and $ 6.6\% $ sea salt. These results demonstrate the feasibility of ML-assisted RF DiSC for rapid, single-particle MP classification in aqueous environments. Future work will focus on improving classification performance through enhanced RF calibration, increased spectral coverage, larger training datasets, and validation using environmentally aged and biologically contaminated microplastics.

</details>


### [270] [When EOS Tokens Disagree: Understanding Length Inflation in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20511)
*Yuxiao Yang,Tianrun Yu,Shangzhe Li,Kaixiang Zhao,Xuchao Zhang,Chetan Bansal,Huaxiu Yao,Taylor W. Killian,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的长度膨胀问题，发现基础学生模型与后训练教师模型之间的终止令牌不匹配是重要诱因。将功能等价的 EOS 令牌统一为共享语义终止动作可显著缓解该问题，但并非全部原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线策略蒸馏中，学生模型的回复会异常变长甚至耗尽生成预算（长度膨胀）。作者希望找出导致这一行为的根本原因，尤其是教师与学生模型在终止行为上的差异。

Method: 作者在 Qwen3、Llama 和 Gemma 三个模型家族上，对比基础学生与后训练教师在终止令牌上的停步概率分布，识别出"终止令牌不匹配"现象；进而提出把功能等价的 EOS 令牌视为共享语义终止动作的处理方式，并通过 K2-Horizon 训练不同阶段的 OPD 分析来跟踪终止偏好随训练演化的规律。

Result: 即使两模型声明的终止集相同，它们也可能把停步概率放在不同的 EOS 令牌上，这种不匹配会抑制学生偏好的终止动作却无法可靠迁移教师偏好的替代方案；仅对齐解码终止集不足，而统一 EOS 令牌语义在三个模型家族上均显著缓解了长度膨胀。分阶段分析显示终止偏好会在训练中大幅漂移，且在 OPD 后期出现一种即使终止对齐后仍持续的长度膨胀。

Conclusion: 终止令牌不匹配是 OPD 长度动态的一个重要但非穷尽的来源；作者发布了包含所提终止处理修正的实现。

Abstract: We study length inflation in on-policy distillation (OPD), where student responses can become excessively long and even exhaust the generation budget. We identify \emph{termination-token mismatch} between base students and post-trained teachers as an important source of this behavior. Across Qwen3, Llama, and Gemma, the two models can place their stopping probability on different EOS tokens, even when their declared stopping sets are identical. This mismatch can suppress the student's preferred termination action without reliably transferring the teacher-preferred alternative. We show that aligning the decoding stopping set alone is insufficient, while treating functionally equivalent EOS tokens as a shared semantic stopping action substantially mitigates mismatch-induced length inflation across all three model families. To further understand how termination behavior evolves over training, we study OPD across different K2-Horizon training stages. This stage-wise analysis shows that termination preferences can shift substantially during training, while also revealing a distinct length inflation late in the OPD run that persists beyond termination alignment. Together, these results identify termination mismatch as an important, but not exhaustive, source of OPD length dynamics. We release an implementation incorporating the proposed termination-handling corrections.

</details>


### [271] [Mitigating Retaliatory Algorithmic Collusion in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20548)
*Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CURB，一种通过在 Q-learning 中惩罚智能体策略跨合作/背叛历史的总变差（TV）距离信号来进行奖励塑形与信念注入的干预框架，可将简单惩罚码（SPC）不动点转化为平凡不动点，从而阻止由惩罚威胁维持的合谋均衡，并在 Bertrand 与 Cournot 重复博弈及深度 Q 网络中显著降低合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体在重复交互中即使无通信、无共享设计，也可能收敛到类似显性合谋的超竞争结果。现有的缓解方法多局限于双边平台、拍卖等特定经济场景，缺乏面向一般重复博弈的通用干预设计。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者将先前 Q-learning 合谋的实证观察与经典简单惩罚码（SPC）理论形式化关联：证明任何非平凡 SPC 都会在智能体策略中诱导出可量化的条件依赖，该依赖可通过智能体在合作历史与背叛历史下动作分布之间的总变差（TV）距离来检测。基于此提出 CURB——一种奖励塑形框架，在 Q-learning 过程中惩罚该 TV 距离信号，从而保证将动力学中的任意 SPC 不动点转化为平凡不动点。此外还构建了信念注入机制。

Result: 在 Bertrand 与 Cournot 竞争型重复博弈中，CURB 大幅减少了 Q-learning 智能体的合谋行为；进一步实验表明 CURB 可扩展到 Bertrand 竞争下的深度 Q 网络（DQN）智能体，暗示该机制不限于表格型 Q-learning。

Conclusion: CURB 提供了一种超越特定经济设定、适用于一般重复博弈的合谋干预方法，通过惩罚 TV 距离信号消除惩罚威胁型合谋均衡，并在多种博弈环境和深度强化学习中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents trained to maximize their own reward in repeated interactions can converge to supra-competitive outcomes resembling explicit collusion, without communication or shared design. Existing mitigation approaches are largely tied to specific economic settings, like two-sided platforms and auctions, leaving open how to design interventions for general repeated games. We address this gap by formalizing the connection between empirical observations from prior work on Q-learning collusion and classical theory of Simple Penal Codes (SPCs). We show any non-trivial SPC induces a quantifiable conditional dependence in agents' policies, detectable via the total variation distance between an agent's action distributions across cooperation and defection histories. Building on this connection, we propose CURB (Collusion Unwinding via Reward shaping and Belief injection), a reward-shaping framework that penalizes this Total Variation (TV) distance signal during Q-learning and is guaranteed to convert any SPC fixed point of the dynamics into a trivial one, thus precluding collusive equilibria sustained by punishment threats. Empirically, CURB substantially reduces collusion by Q-learning agents in both Bertrand and Cournot Competition Repeated Games. We further demonstrate that CURB extends to deep Q-network agents in Bertrand competition, suggesting the mechanism generalizes beyond tabular Q-learning.

</details>


### [272] [CrystalMO-TuRBO: Multi-Objective Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization for High-precision Joint Crystal Structure Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20592)
*Joseph Agada,Yishu Wang,Arpan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CrystalMO-TuRBO，一种面向晶体结构精修的多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化架构，将 X 射线与中子衍射残差建模为独立目标，并通过两阶段优化（全局探索 + 局部精修）实现联合精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体结构精修是材料表征中的基础反问题。传统最小二乘与似然优化依赖局部搜索，难以应对非凸、含噪、强相关的参数空间；X 射线与中子联合精修因二者互补但相互竞争的灵敏度，常通过需要人工加权的标量化目标合并，导致次优解。

Method: 提出 CrystalMO-TuRBO：将 X 射线与中子残差作为独立目标，转化为归一化最大化问题；采用两阶段策略——第一阶段用并行信赖域贝叶斯优化在多个标量化上进行全局探索以定位有前景区域，第二阶段在收缩区域内做局部精修以获得高精度解，从而显式分离全局搜索与细粒度优化。

Result: 在单晶 Ho2Ti2O7 的实验 X 射线与中子衍射数据上评估，相比经典精修方法和贝叶斯优化基线，该方法在收敛性、鲁棒性和参数精度上均有提升。

Conclusion: CrystalMO-TuRBO 通过多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化和两阶段全局-局部分离策略，有效解决了 X 射线与中子联合精修中的人工加权与局部最优问题，在单晶烧绿石体系上实现了更优的收敛、鲁棒性与参数精度。

Abstract: Crystal structure refinement is a fundamental inverse problem in materials characterization, where structural parameters are optimized to reproduce experimental diffraction data. Conventional approaches, such as least-squares and likelihood-based optimization, rely on local search and often struggle with non-convex, noisy, and highly correlated parameter landscapes, particularly when integrating multiple diffraction modalities. Joint refinement of X-ray and neutron data is especially challenging due to their complementary but competing sensitivities, which are typically combined through scalarized objectives requiring manual weighting and leading to suboptimal solutions. We propose CrystalMO-TuRBO, a multi-objective trust region Bayesian optimization architecture for joint crystal structure refinement. The method models X-ray and neutron discrepancies as separate objectives and transforms the problem into a normalized maximization setting. A two-phase optimization strategy is introduced: Phase 1 performs global exploration using parallel trust-region Bayesian optimization across multiple scalarizations to identify promising regions of the parameter space, while Phase 2 conducts localized refinement within a shrinking region to achieve high-precision solutions. This design explicitly separates global search from fine-grained optimization, addressing the unique accuracy requirements of refinement tasks. We evaluate the proposed method on experimentally collected X-ray and neutron diffraction data from single-crystal Ho2Ti2O7. Results demonstrate improved convergence, robustness, and parameter precision compared to classical refinement methods and Bayesian optimization baselines on refinement of a single-crystal pyrochlore material system.

</details>


### [273] [Recursive Quantum Long Short-Term Memory for Stable Short-Horizon Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20594)
*Mu-En Lee,Yen-Ku Liu,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了递归QLSTM与标准QLSTM在日最低/最高温预测上的性能，发现递归结构更早收敛、误差更低、泛化差距更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLSTM模型的优化行为会因随机初始化和时间上下文的不同而大幅波动，因此需要探索更稳定的架构来提升紧凑型混合量子-经典时序模型的可靠性。

Method: 采用多伦多每日天气观测数据，在相同训练设置下，对递归QLSTM与标准QLSTM进行一天前温度预测对比；分别在8、16和32天三种输入窗口长度下，各运行20个随机种子，比较收敛速度、预测精度与泛化能力。

Result: 递归模型始终更早达到近最优测试损失，降低了平均绝对误差（MAE）和均方根误差（RMSE），并表现出更小的泛化差距。

Conclusion: 递归量子特征变换能够提升紧凑型混合量子-经典时序模型的稳定性和样本外预测性能。

Abstract: Quantum long short-term memory (QLSTM) models extend recurrent sequence learning with variational quantum circuits, but their optimization behavior can vary substantially across random initializations and temporal contexts. This paper evaluates a recursive QLSTM architecture against a standard QLSTM for one-step-ahead prediction of daily minimum and maximum temperature. Using daily weather observations from Toronto and identical training settings, we compare convergence, predictive accuracy, and generalization across input windows of 8, 16, and 32 days over 20 random seeds. The recursive model consistently reaches a near-optimal test loss earlier, reduces mean absolute error and root mean squared error, and exhibits a smaller generalization gap. These results indicate that recursive quantum feature transformations can improve stability and out-of-sample performance for compact hybrid quantum--classical temporal models.

</details>


### [274] [COIN-GP: Cooperative Online Learning in Networked Distributed Systems with Partial Measurements via Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20598)
*Zewen Yang,Xiaobing Dai,Zhenxiao Yin,Hang Zhao,Zhijun Li,C. C. Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于观测器的动态协作学习框架，结合在线分布式高斯过程回归，用于在分布式传感器网络中仅部分状态可观测的条件下，联合估计系统状态与部分未知动态，并给出数据采集策略与误差上界，仿真验证其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式传感器网络中，系统状态与部分未知动态的联合估计面临两大挑战：测量信息不完整（仅有部分状态可观）以及GP模型不完备（deficient GP models）。现有分布式GP方法难以在测量缺失和模型不足的情况下保证准确的估计性能。

Method: 提出基于观测器的动态协作学习框架，集成在线分布式高斯过程(GP)回归；各节点通过观测器与协作机制共享信息以补偿部分状态不可观测的问题；设计新的数据采集策略并给出保证数据获取可行性的理论条件。

Result: 推导出涵盖状态估计与模型估计的统一误差上界，该上界借助高斯过程的确定性误差界得出；经验仿真表明所提方法在估计精度上优于现有分布式GP-based方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够在部分观测与GP模型不完备的情况下实现状态与未知动态的精确联合估计，具有明确的理论误差保证，并通过仿真验证了其相对现有分布式GP方法的优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of jointly estimating the system states and partially unknown dynamics within distributed sensor-equipped networks, particularly in scenarios where only partial state observations are available. To address this issue, we propose an observer-based dynamic cooperative learning framework incorporating online distributed Gaussian Process (GP) regression, which enables accurate estimation despite incomplete in measurements and deficient GP models. In addition, a novel data collection strategy is introduced, with theoretical conditions ensuring feasible data acquisition. Moreover, we also derive an error upper bound encompassing state estimation and model estimation, leveraging the deterministic error bounds of GPs. Empirical simulations demonstrate the superiority of our approach compared to existing distributed GP-based methods.

</details>


### [275] [Multi-center Medical Data Mining with FL-Net - A One-stop Shop for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20650)
*Simon Süwer,Julian Klemm,Elisa Acitelli,Mathieu Almeida,Lucia Altucci,Zsolt Bagyura,Michelangela Barbieri,Zsolt-Zoltán Bedő,Rosaria Benedetti,Béla Bihari,Csongor Csalóka,Lucia Dicunta,Stanislav Ehrlich,Bjoern M. Eskofier,Sándor-József Fejér,Georg Fröwis,Walter Hötzendorfer,Alexandra Kautzky-Willer,Jens Johann Georg Lohmann,Marianna Maranghi,Lorenzo Marconi,Rudolf Mayer,Wouter Leonard Megchelenbrink,Monika Moga,Adham Mottalib,Sanjeev Mehta,Madeleine Müller,Thomas Nyström,Balázs-Attila Orbán,Paul O'Toole,Giuseppe Paolisso,Paolo Parini,Matteo Pedrelli,Enrico Petrillo,Philipp Poindl,Niklas Probul,Anastasia Pustozerova,Tanja Šarčević,Lukas Weilguny,Jan Baumbach,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FL-Net，一个面向联邦临床研究的新型框架，通过模块化数据协调、数据发现、披露控制、版本化工具和容器化工作流执行，满足联邦学习中既往框架未能全部实现的五项需求，并在多达 50 个并发客户端的场景下完成端到端验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可在不共享患者级数据的前提下实现协作训练，但现有研究大多停留在模拟阶段，缺乏可落地、可复现且隐私安全的真实临床研究框架。作者从文献中归纳出五项需求，并发现 14 个现有联邦学习框架均未能完全满足这些需求，因此需要一个真正实用的解决方案。

Method: FL-Net 通过集成模块化数据协调（data harmonization）、数据发现（data discovery）、披露控制（disclosure control）、安全构建的版本化 FL-Net-Tools，以及容器化联邦工作流执行，构建了一个持久化网络，支持在多个研究之间复用已协调的数据与工作流。

Result: FL-Net 的端到端能力通过数据协调、跨 MIMIC 与 US-130 的跨研究患者发现、以及多达 50 个并发客户端的可复现且可审计的联邦工作流得到验证。该框架正在 dAIbetes 与 Microb-AI-ome 两个欧盟项目中开发，将覆盖 9 个国家 10 家医院、超过 80 万名患者，涵盖纵向与单时间点数据。

Conclusion: FL-Net 为可互操作、可复现且保护隐私的多中心临床研究提供了实用的基础，填补了现有联邦学习研究停留在模拟、无法满足真实临床需求之间的空白。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training without sharing patient-level data, but most studies remain simulations. Based on five requirements derived from the literature, we analyzed 14 FL frameworks and found that none fully satisfied these requirements. We present FL-Net, a novel federated clinical research framework to fulfill all requirements. It integrates modular data harmonization, data discovery, disclosure control, securely built versioned FL-Net-Tools and containerized federated workflow execution into a persistent network. It enables the re-use of harmonized data and workflows across studies. FL-Net's end-to-end capabilities were evaluated through harmonization, cross-study patient discovery across MIMIC and US-130, and reproducible, audited federated workflows with up to 50 concurrent clients. FL-Net is being developed within the dAIbetes and Microb-AI-ome EU projects and will cover over 800,000 patients across 10 hospitals in 9 countries covering longitudinal and single point in time data, FL-Net provides a practical foundation for interoperable, reproducible, and privacy-preserving multicenter clinical research.

</details>


### [276] [RISC-V and machine learning: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20677)
*Shriman Keshri,Apparna Singh,Chinmaya Kumar Palo,Shreya Adya,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了 RISC-V 指令集架构在机器学习领域的应用现状，涵盖学术与商业实现、软件框架及实际应用，提出统一分类法，并指出标准化、验证复杂性和生态碎片化等挑战，最终给出四个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源处理器架构与机器学习的交叉融合，推动了对可定制、高效且易获取硬件的需求；作为开源 ISA 的 RISC-V 有望成为机器学习系统的基石，但其能力、挑战与方向缺乏系统梳理。

Method: 采用调查综述方法，分析近期研究中的学术与商业实现、软件框架和实际应用；从指令集扩展、核心实现、编译器优化到部署策略对 RISC-V 机器学习生态进行系统评估；提出统一分类法、性能与设计权衡对比、工具链成熟度评估，并识别指令集扩展与专用加速器的新兴趋势。

Result: 发现 RISC-V 在能效、专用指令开发和框架集成方面取得进展，但仍面临标准化不足、验证复杂性和生态碎片化等挑战；提出四个研究方向：专用神经处理扩展、自适应模块化处理器架构、安全框架以及高能效多域架构。

Conclusion: 四项研究方向为将 RISC-V 打造为下一代机器学习系统的基础平台提供了路线图，有助于解决当前限制并推动该领域的持续发展。

Abstract: The intersection of open-source processor architectures and machine learning is driving the demand for customizable, efficient, and accessible hardware. This survey examines the state of the RISC-V ISA in machine learning applications, analyzing current capabilities, challenges, and future directions based on recent research. The analysis covers academic and commercial implementations, software frameworks, and real-world applications. The RISC-V machine learning ecosystem is evaluated, from instruction set extensions and core implementations to compiler optimizations and deployment strategies. Key contributions include a unified taxonomy of RISC-V ML implementations, a comparative analysis of performance and design trade-offs, an evaluation of software toolchain maturity, and the identification of emerging trends in instruction set extensions and specialized accelerators. Findings reveal progress in energy efficiency, specialized instruction development, and framework integration, while highlighting challenges in standardization, verification complexity, and ecosystem fragmentation. The analysis proposes four research directions to address current limitations: specialized neural processing extensions, adaptive and modular processor architectures, security frameworks, and energy-efficient multi-domain architectures. These directions provide a roadmap for advancing RISC-V as a foundational platform for next-generation machine learning systems.

</details>


### [277] [Video DeltaNet: A Video-Native Hybrid Attention for Livestream Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20744)
*Haocheng Xi,Yiming Xie,Hexu Zhao,Yiwen Zhang,Michael Liu,Thomas Creavin,Kurt Keutzer,Xiuyu Li,Zhaoyang Lv,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video DeltaNet (VDN)，通过将局部 Softmax 注意力与双向线性记忆（Video Delta Attention, VDA）相结合来加速视频扩散模型，在极小性能损失下实现 14.5 倍去噪加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在去噪过程中需反复处理长时空 token 序列，注意力成为主要计算瓶颈。线性注意力虽高效（已在 LLM 中广泛应用），但直接用于视频模型会丧失高质量生成所需的细粒度交互，因此需要一种兼顾效率与质量的混合方案。

Method: 提出 VDN：① 混合架构——局部 Softmax 注意力负责细粒度交互，双向线性记忆负责长程视频上下文；② Video Delta Attention (VDA)——线性分支按帧更新记忆，并联合融入该帧的空间 token；③ 双分支通过独立的输出投影和可学习门控进行校准；④ staged teacher-alignment 蒸馏配方，将新通路渐进引入预训练模型；⑤ 在 MiniMax H3 上实例化，视频-视频交互采用混合注意力，文本/音频交互保留 Softmax；⑥ 配合八步蒸馏与优化后的 SGLang 服务栈。

Result: 在八张 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上，VDN-H3 对 14.3 秒、768p 视频完成 DiT 去噪仅需 6.70 秒；相比同 GPU 数目下 50 步 dense H3 基线，实现 14.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过线性记忆与 Softmax 注意力的混合及渐进式教师对齐训练，VDN 在保持视频生成质量的同时，大幅降低注意力计算开销，为长视频扩散模型的高效部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Video diffusion models repeatedly process long spatiotemporal token sequences during denoising, making attention a major computational bottleneck. Linear attention offers an appealing alternative and has been widely adopted in recent large language models, but directly applying it to video models often fails to preserve the fine-grained interactions required for high-quality generation. We present Video DeltaNet (VDN), which combines local Softmax attention with bidirectional linear memory for long-range video context. Its linear branch introduces Video Delta Attention (VDA), which updates memory once per frame by jointly incorporating its spatial tokens. Separate output projections and learnable gates calibrate the two branches, while a staged teacher-alignment recipe progressively introduces the new pathway into pretrained models. We instantiate VDN on MiniMax H3, applying the hybrid to video-to-video interactions while retaining Softmax for interactions involving text or audio. With eight-step distillation and an optimized SGLang serving stack, VDN-H3 completes DiT denoising for a 14.3-second, 768p video in 6.70 seconds on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs, corresponding to a 14.5x speedup over the 50-step dense H3 baseline on the same GPU count.

</details>


### [278] [Calibrated RF-Fingerprinting Under Interference With Heterogeneous Transmission Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20765)
*Tariq Abdul-Quddoos,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在多发射机同频同时发射（同信道干扰）场景下的射频指纹识别，将其建模为多标签分类问题，采用一维CNN并引入置信度校准，以保证误报漏检（假阴性）数量的统计上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频指纹识别研究几乎只考虑单一发射机工作的场景，限制了其在实际频谱环境中的适用性。真实无线环境中常存在同信道干扰，即多个信号在时间与频率上重叠、相互干扰，因此需要研究在该类高竞争环境下的发射机识别问题。

Method: 将问题建模为多标签分类，使用一维卷积神经网络（1D CNN）识别同时发射的多个发射机。在此基础上对模型进行置信度校准，推导标签概率的置信阈值，保证假阴性（漏检）平均数量的上界，从而为“不遗漏真实频谱违规”提供置信度保障。

Result: 在POWDER 5G测试床上，使用发射802.11a（Wi-Fi）、4G LTE和5G NR波形的真实设备数据验证。结果显示校准后精度最高达97%、最低为73%（取决于信道条件）；针对不同假阴性上界进行校准后，微观召回率约为(1 - 校准假阴性)；且校准对分布外干扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在真实高竞争无线环境中展现出潜力，能够在存在同信道干扰的情况下进行可靠的射频指纹识别，并通过对假阴性上界的控制提供可量化的置信度保障。

Abstract: Radio Frequency(RF)-Fingerprinting is a spectrum monitoring technique that identifies specific transmitters based on hardware impairments imprinted within the emitted signal. Although widely researched, studies almost exclusively consider scenarios where only one transmitter is emitting at a time, limiting real world applicability. In this work, we further the study of RF-Fingerprinting by considering co-channel interference, with multiple emitted signals interfering with each other, overlapping in time and frequency. Specifically, we formulate this problem as a multi-label classification problem and employ a 1D convolutional neural network (CNN). Furthermore, the models are calibrated such that the confidence thresholds for the label probabilities are derived, with guarantees on the upper bound on the average number of False Negatives, providing a degree of confidence in not missing a true spectrum policy violation. The proposed method is validated using real world data from the POWDER 5G testbed on devices transmitting 802.11a(Wi-Fi), 4G LTE, and 5G NR waveforms. The results show accuracy as high as 97% and as low as 73% after calibration depending on channel conditions. Also calibrating for various average false negatives upper bounds achieves micro recall scores of approximately (1 - calibrated false negatives) with the calibration robust to out-of-distribution interference, demonstrating the potential of the proposed method in a realistic high contention wireless environment

</details>


### [279] [PosteriorBench: From Point Estimates to Posterior Matching in Evaluating Generative Inverse Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20794)
*Jiachen Yao,Zi-Siang Hsu,Xi Deng,Aditi Gupta,Xin Ju,Sally M Benson,Gege Wen,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PosteriorBench 是一个用于评估生成式逆问题求解器“分布级精度”的基准，涵盖四个物理反问题、高保真参考后验与五项评估指标，揭示现有方法在分布匹配上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式逆问题求解器的评估过于关注能否生成单个合理重建，但病态（ill-posed）问题中多个解可能与同一稀疏/噪声观测相容。仅看点估计精度会掩盖模式坍缩、过度自信的不确定性或对不相容解的错误平均等问题，因此需要直接评估方法是否恢复真实后验分布。

Method: 提出 PosteriorBench 基准：选取四个基于物理的反问题（Darcy 流反演、Poisson 源恢复、碳捕集与封存、光传输材料推断）；用拒绝采样与 MCMC 等计算昂贵但可靠的流程构建高保真参考后验；设计五指标后验评估套件——后验均值误差、后验标准差误差、最大均值差异（MMD）、切片 Wasserstein 距离、径向平均功率谱误差，分别衡量点估计精度、边际不确定性、分布对齐与全局频率保真度；覆盖稀疏传感、低分辨率观测、非线性正演、不同噪声水平与多模态先验，并提供统一的分布匹配与不确定性量化流水线。

Result: 实验显示当前求解器在四个任务上都存在显著的分布匹配差距；神经算子能提升对分辨率的鲁棒性；引导权重（guidance weights）与生成噪声是校准后验方差的关键因素。

Conclusion: 仅靠点估计精度不足以评估生成式逆问题求解器，PosteriorBench 通过高保真参考后验和多项分布级指标揭示了现有方法在分布匹配上的不足，并指出神经算子、引导权重与生成噪声对改善后验精度与方差校准的重要性。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to solve scientific inverse problems, but existing evaluations still focus primarily on whether a method can produce a single plausible reconstruction. This is insufficient for ill-posed problems, where multiple solutions may be consistent with the same sparse or noisy observations. In these settings, a method can achieve strong pointwise accuracy while still failing to capture the true posterior through mode collapse, overconfident uncertainty, or averaging incompatible solutions. We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for evaluating the distributional accuracy of generative inverse solvers. PosteriorBench evaluates four physics-based inverse problems: Darcy flow inversion, Poisson source recovery, carbon capture and storage, and light transport material inference. For each task, we construct high-fidelity reference posteriors using computationally heavy but established procedures such as rejection sampling and Markov chain Monte Carlo, enabling direct assessment of whether solvers recover the full set of solutions rather than the single best sample. We pair these references with a five-metric posterior evaluation suite: posterior-mean error, posterior-standard-deviation error, maximum mean discrepancy, sliced Wasserstein distance, and radially averaged power-spectrum error. These metrics assess pointwise accuracy, marginal uncertainty, distributional alignment, and global frequency fidelity. The benchmark spans sparse sensing, low-resolution observations, nonlinear forward models, varying noise levels, and multimodal priors, with a unified pipeline for distribution matching and uncertainty quantification. Our experiments reveal substantial distribution-matching gaps across current solvers, while showing that neural operators improve resolution robustness, and guidance weights and generation noise are key to posterior-variance calibration.

</details>


### [280] [Score Centering Stabilizes Off-policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20807)
*Martin Marek,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"分数居中"(score centering)的加性修正项，通过抵消训练与推理引擎间的漂移来稳定大规模语言模型的强化学习，可单独或与重要性采样结合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习对训练引擎与推理引擎之间的微小差异(即训练-推理不匹配,TIM)极为敏感，而完全消除TIM不切实际且会严重损失采样(rollout)效率。作者发现TIM导致RL不稳定的关键在于"漂移"(drift)——一种随每个训练步累积的、训练与推理引擎间的持续性偏差。

Method: 作者推导出一个加性的"分数居中"修正项，通过抵消漂移来在TIM条件下稳定RL。该方法可单独使用，且由于修正是加性的，可与重要性采样(importance sampling)方法相组合。

Result: 在训练参数量从0.6B到30B的模型中，仅靠分数居中即可在量化条件下达到或超过基于重要性采样的方法，且随不匹配程度加剧差距扩大。此外，分数居中与重要性采样的组合在"陈旧性"(staleness)实验中优于纯重要性采样基线。

Conclusion: 分数居中是一种简单有效的加性修正，能够在不牺牲采样效率的前提下稳定TIM下的RL训练，并可与重要性采样叠加实现更优性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models is notoriously sensitive to small differences between training and inference engines, often referred to as the training-inference mismatch (TIM). However, completely eliminating TIM is impractical, as it would come at a major cost to rollout efficiency. In this paper, we show that the instability of RL under TIM is primarily caused by drift: a persistent bias between training and inference engines that accumulates with every training step. We derive an additive "score centering" correction term that stabilizes RL under TIM by canceling drift. When training models from 0.6B to 30B parameters, score centering alone matches or outperforms methods based on importance sampling under quantization, with the gap growing as the mismatch becomes more severe. Because the correction is additive, score centering also composes with importance sampling -- their composition outperforms pure importance-sampling baselines in our staleness experiments.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [An absolute measure of heterogeneity for lnOR in meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19211)
*Ke Yang,Lei Shi,Wangli Xu,Liping Zhu,Tiejun Tong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对二元结局的荟萃分析，本文提出了一种与样本量无关的异质性度量 ICC_MA^OR，并给出其估计量 I^2_A，以克服传统 I^2 随样本量增大趋近于 1 的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的异质性统计量 I^2 高度依赖各研究的样本量，并随样本量增大而趋于 1，导致异质性被高估；而新近提出的样本量无关度量 ICC_MA 仅适用于连续型结局。现实中二元结局更为常见，且常以对数优势比（lnOR）作为效应量，因此缺乏适用于二元结局的样本量无关异质性度量。

Method: 通过引入潜在连续变量模型，在随机效应荟萃分析框架下，以对数优势比（lnOR）为效应量，推导出具有闭式表达形式的样本量无关异质性度量 ICC_MA^OR，并提出其新估计量 I^2_A。通过模拟研究和真实数据应用对其进行验证。

Result: 模拟研究表明，新估计量 I^2_A 近似无偏，并且当连续变量可观测时，与应用于标准化均值差的对应度量高度一致。真实数据应用进一步显示，所提出的二元结局异质性统计量与连续结局对应度量在不同结局类型下给出可解释且一致的估计。

Conclusion: 本文填补了二元结局荟萃分析中样本量无关异质性度量的空白，提出的 ICC_MA^OR 及其估计量 I^2_A 表现良好，可为不同结局类型的荟萃分析提供一致且可解释的异质性评估。

Abstract: Quantifying the heterogeneity is an important issue in meta-analysis. The conventional heterogeneity statistic, $I^2$, heavily depends on study sample sizes and tends to approach one as the sample sizes increase. To overcome this limitation, a sample size independent measure, ${\rm ICC}_{\rm MA}$, has recently been developed to quantify the heterogeneity, yet this method is restricted to meta-analysis with continuous outcomes. In practice, however, binary outcomes are more widely encountered, with the log odds ratio (lnOR) frequently adopted as an effect size to compare the effects of two treatments in terms of odds. To fill the gap, we propose a new heterogeneity measure for meta-analysis with binary outcomes by introducing a latent continuous variable model. Under this framework, the proposed measure admits a closed-form expression within the random-effects meta-analysis model with lnOR as the effect size. We define this sample size independent measure as ${\rm ICC}_{\rm MA}^{\rm OR}$, and moreover propose a new estimator, $I^2_A$, to evaluate this measure. Simulation studies demonstrate that our new estimator is nearly unbiased and aligns closely with its counterpart applied for standardized mean differences when the continuous variable is observable. A real data application further demonstrates that the proposed heterogeneity statistic for binary outcomes and its counterpart for continuous outcomes provide interpretable and coherent estimates across different outcome types.

</details>


### [282] [When a High Score Is an Illusion: Certifying Genuine versus Repackaged Forecasting Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19223)
*Pin Ni,Francesca Medda,Ramin Okhrati*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究排名对比中的参考（baseline）复用问题，证明在独立训练条件下，若每个地图对的加权参考重叠为零，则可在任意地图与法则下保持总体排名对比的关联性；并提出无偏三轨迹核估计与完整 U 统计量估计交互项。在北京空气质量数据上，交互项占七个学习预测期望共享得分的 91.4%–94.5%，匹配类别对照实验显示区分参考可将显著拒绝数从 402 降至 41。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排名依赖用于比较的观测值，复用这些观测会在被评估预测与结果固定不变的情况下，人为引入预测排名对比与结果排名对比之间的关联。这种由参考复用造成的虚假关联会扭曲评分与推断，因此需要刻画并消除该效应。

Method: 刻画能保持指定总体排名对比（如预测排名减基线排名、结果排名减基线排名）之间关联的参考分配。在独立训练条件下，整条轨迹独立同分布抽样、轨迹内部依赖不受限；将期望得分分解为目标项与地图对之间的显式交互项；重分配参考时固定已学地图、参考法则与系数行和；以每个地图对的加权参考重叠为零作为一致保持的充要条件。构造无偏三轨迹核估计交互项，用独立评估与验证给出有限样本下界；当所有抽取可重组时，用完整 U 统计量直接估计同一目标。引入尖锐的共享基线区间（含并列情形）进一步收紧两种构造。

Result: 北京空气质量档案中，交互项占七个学习预测在经验档案法则下期望共享得分的 91.4%–94.5%；匹配类别对照零实验中，区分参考将 1000 个面板中的拒绝数从 402 降至 41。另有针对类别拟合误差与时间反馈的独立结论。

Conclusion: 参考复用会系统性引入预测与结果排名对比之间的虚假关联，是期望共享得分的主导成分；通过强制每个地图对的加权参考重叠为零可一致地消除该效应，并且可通过三轨迹核或完整 U 统计量对其进行无偏估计与有限样本检验。该方法在空气质量预测的数据化实验中显著减少了虚假显著结果。

Abstract: Ranks depend on the observations used for comparison. Reusing those observations can add association between forecast and outcome rank contrasts even when the evaluated forecast and outcome stay fixed. We characterize assignments that preserve association between specified population-rank contrasts, including forecast rank minus baseline rank compared with outcome rank minus baseline rank. Conditional on independent training, whole trajectories are sampled independently from a common law, with unrestricted dependence within each trajectory. The expected score separates into its target and an explicit interaction between map pairs. When reassigning references, we keep the learned maps, reference law and coefficient row sums fixed. Zero weighted reference overlap for every map pair is necessary and sufficient for preservation uniformly over permitted maps and laws. An unbiased three-trajectory kernel estimates the interaction; independent evaluation and validation provide finite-sample lower bounds. Complete U-statistics estimate the same target directly when all draws can be recombined. Sharp shared-baseline ranges, including ties, tighten both constructions. In a Beijing air-quality archive, interaction accounts for 91.4% to 94.5% of seven learned forecasts' expected shared scores under the empirical archive law. Separate results address category-fitting error and temporal feedback. In a matched category-control null experiment, distinct references reduce rejections from 402 to 41 out of 1,000 panels.

</details>


### [283] [Binning-Independent Bayesian Analysis of Time-Dependent Perturbed Angular Distribution data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19227)
*Franziskus von Spee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需对时间数据分箱、直接基于单事件条件概率构造似然并用贝叶斯框架推断 g 因子的新方法，模拟分析显示其比传统分箱方法在低统计量数据下更可靠，但后验概率常呈多峰，不能用单一高斯近似概括。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析时间相关微扰角分布（TDPAD）数据时需对时间数据分箱，这会带来信息损失，尤其在低统计量情况下可靠性下降，因此需要一种避免分箱、能更充分利用单事件信息的方法来推断 g 因子。

Method: 利用单事件的条件概率构造似然函数，完全避免对时间数据的分箱；将该似然置于贝叶斯框架中，通过计算目标 g 因子的后验概率密度函数进行参数推断。

Result: 用模拟数据集对比新方法与基于分箱数据的传统方法，发现新方法的后验概率密度在多情况下呈现多峰结构，不能用单一高斯近似简洁概括；同时新方法在低统计量数据集上得到的结果更加可靠。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于非分箱单事件似然的贝叶斯方法在低统计量数据分析中优于传统分箱方法，但后验分布的多峰性要求研究者放弃单一高斯近似、采用更完整的后验表征来解读结果。

Abstract: We propose a new approach for analyzing Time-Dependent Perturbed Angular Distribution data. In this, a likelihood is constructed with the help of conditional probabilities of single events and binning of time data is avoided. This likelihood is used in a Bayesian framework to calculate the posterior probability density function for the $g$ factor of interest. This approach is compared to more traditional approaches that use binned data by analyzing simulated datasets. In many cases the resulting posterior probability densities are observed to be multimodal and the results can thus often not be summarized with a single Gaussian approximation. We find that results from the new approach are more reliable for low-statistics datasets.

</details>


### [284] [Laplacian U-Processes for Multiple Change-Point Detection in Dependent Text Networks: An Application to Historical Chinese Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19306)
*Fanghua Chen,Yizhou Cai,Lu zhou,Ting Fung Ma*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出双视角加权一致性算子（WCO）框架，用于弱依赖文本网络中的离线变点检测。该方法通过加权词共现图构造一阶直接共现与二阶共享上下文两种视角，并利用独立试点语料学得的映射投影到成对欧氏观测，由 WCO 扫描检测跨视角依赖变化，辅以依赖乘子自助法与分段块自助法进行检验与定位，并在《新青年》语料上检测到 1919 年 11 月的变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱依赖文本网络中的变点（change-point）检测缺乏能够刻画词之间一阶与二阶关系、并对跨视角依赖变化敏感的统一统计框架；同时需要提供具有渐近保障的检验与定位方法，以应用于历史文本语言学转型等实际场景。

Method: 提出双视角加权一致性算子（WCO）框架。先由加权词共现图得到一阶直接共现与二阶共享上下文两种视角，再用独立试点语料学到的映射将两视角投影为成对欧氏观测；WCO 扫描检测跨视角依赖变化。推断方面使用依赖乘子自助法（dependent multiplier bootstrap）进行变点检验，采用分段块自助法（segment-wise block bootstrap）给出变点位置的描述性稳定区间；并以 CUSUM 与高斯核 MMD 扫描作为补充基准。

Result: 理论上建立了零假设下的弱收敛、自助法有效性、渐近尺寸控制，在单一可识别变点下的相合定位，以及词元级误差的扰动界。实证中，在 1915–1921 年《新青年》语料上检测到 1919 年 11 月发生变点，描述性 95% 稳定区间为 1919 年 4 月至 1920 年 6 月，与五四运动及新文化运动前后的语言转型相一致。

Conclusion: 所提 WCO 框架为弱依赖文本网络提供了一套理论上可验证、实践上可操作的离线变点检测方案，既具备渐近有效性与定位相合性，又能在真实历史语料中给出与已知历史事件吻合的变点定位与稳定区间。

Abstract: We propose a two-view weighted-concordance operator (WCO) framework for offline change-point detection in weakly dependent text networks. Weighted word-co-occurrence graphs yield first-order direct-co-occurrence and second-order shared-context views, projected into paired Euclidean observations using maps learned from an independent pilot corpus. A WCO scan detects changes in cross-view dependence. A dependent multiplier bootstrap tests for a change, while a segment-wise block bootstrap gives a descriptive stability interval for its location. We establish null weak convergence, bootstrap validity, asymptotic size control, consistent localization under a single identifiable change, and perturbation bounds for token-level errors. CUSUM and Gaussian-kernel MMD scans provide complementary benchmarks. In the 1915-1921 New Youth corpus, the method detects a change in November 1919, with a descriptive 95 percent stability interval from April 1919 to June 1920, consistent with the linguistic transition surrounding China's May Fourth and New Culture movements.

</details>


### [285] [When are time-to-event models a waste of time? Bridging mixture cure models and positive-unlabeled learning for binary classification under right-censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19370)
*Qin Weng,Matthew M. Engelhard*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在右删失临床数据中预测二分类结局时，混合治愈模型（MCM）与正-未标注（PU）学习之间的关系。作者证明两者优化相同的似然函数，区别在于对易感患者在删失前发生事件的概率（即标注倾向）的约束方式；MCM可视作将标注倾向参数化为时间-事件分布的PU模型，从而具备可识别性。通过系统模拟和两个真实临床队列，作者发现仅在无法识别精确删失机制的选择性删失情况下MCM才占优，其余情况下PU学习性能相当且避免了时间-事件方法带来的挑战与偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中右删失使得无法区分"事件永不发生"（阴性/非易感）与"在删失时间后才发生"（阳性/易感）的个体。时间-事件（TTE）方法需要事件时间戳，但时间戳可能不可靠、获取成本高或因临床护理差异而产生偏倚。作者旨在为在MCM与PU学习框架之间做出选择提供循证建议，厘清两者的理论联系，并明确TTE成分何时真正有益于二分类预测。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明MCM与PU学习优化共同的似然函数，差异在于对易感患者在删失前发生事件概率（即PU学习中的标注倾向）的约束方式，并指出MCM可视作将标注倾向参数化为TTE分布的PU模型，从而获得可识别性。随后，通过系统性模拟和两个真实世界临床队列，考察TTE成分何时以及是否有利于二分类预测，并检验不同删失机制（如选择性删失、可识别删失机制等）下的表现。

Result: 模拟与真实数据结果显示，MCM仅在一种特定情形下优于PU学习：即在选择性删失存在、且无法识别控制删失的确切特征时。除此之外，PU学习能达到与MCM相当的性能，同时避免TTE方法带来的挑战和潜在偏倚。

Conclusion: MCM与PU学习本质上是同一似然优化的不同参数化约束方式。除非处于无法识别删失特征的选择性删失情形，否则应优先考虑PU学习，因其性能相当且更简单、更少偏倚。这为临床结局预测中选用何种建模框架提供了明确的循证指导。

Abstract: In clinical settings, predicting binary outcomes is often complicated by right-censoring, which prevents us from distinguishing individuals in whom the event never occurs (i.e., negative, or non-susceptible) from those where it occurs after the censoring time (i.e., positive, or susceptible). To discriminate between these subpopulations, we can use mixture cure models (MCMs), which model both the outcome probability over an infinite horizon and its time-to-event (TTE) distribution when observed. However, the TTE approach requires event timestamps, which can be unreliable, costly to obtain, or biased due to disparities in clinical care. Alternatively, the task can be formulated as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, in which individuals without an observed event are grouped as "unlabeled" rather than negative, recognizing that this group includes positives whose event occurred after censoring. Here we analyze the relationship between these frameworks and provide evidence-based recommendations for choosing between them. We begin by showing that both families optimize a shared likelihood but differ in how they constrain the probability of event occurrence prior to censoring among susceptible patients, which in PU learning is known as the labeling propensity; and that an MCM may be viewed as a PU model whose labeling propensity is parameterized as a TTE distribution, which makes the model identifiable. In systematic simulations and two real-world clinical cohorts, we examine whether and when including the TTE component benefits binary classification. We show that MCMs are preferred in only one specific regime: under selective censoring where the exact features governing censoring cannot be identified. Otherwise, PU learning achieves equivalent performance while avoiding challenges and potential biases associated with the TTE approach.

</details>


### [286] [Qualify-Then-Borrow: A Five-Step Framework for Bayesian Borrowing Beyond Outcome Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19382)
*Haitao Pan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: QTB（Qualify-Then-Borrow）是一种五步决策架构，在动态贝叶斯借用外部信息之前，先通过科学来源评估对数据进行资格判定（合格、可修复、不合格），再据此决定直接使用、修复或排除，最后才让动态借用方法作用于残余的经验兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态贝叶斯借用方法通常会根据当前数据与外部信息的一致程度自动调整借用强度，但仅凭结局数据无法判断外部信息是否真正适合借用。作者指出，需要一种显式的架构，把科学的来源评估与统计的兼容性判断区分开来，避免在存在已知实质性差异（如终点定义问题、人群漂移）时仍机械地借用外部数据，从而导致偏倚和覆盖率下降。

Method: 提出五步流程：1）定义目标；2）评估外部数据；3）将其分类为合格、可修复或不合格；4）分别直接使用、修复后使用或排除；5）再应用选定的贝叶斯借用方法。动态借用仅作为最后一步，在已处理可识别的实质性差异之后，作用于残余的经验兼容性上。通过 82 个预设场景、每个场景 10,000 次重复的模拟进行评估。

Result: 仅凭结局数据无法确定正确的借用决策：两个外部结局分布相同的场景，由于其中一个存在已知终点问题，在稳健混合模型下行为相似但 QTB 决策不同。误分类数据被完全合并时，95% 覆盖率降至 60.8%；稳健混合降权可将覆盖率提高到 91.8%，但仍留下 -4.8 个百分点的偏倚。对测得的人群偏移进行修复可将绝对偏倚从 1.94 降至 0.80 个百分点。在强负向残余漂移下，尽管进行了降权，I 类错误仍达 10.0%。当数据完全兼容时，QTB 退化为通常的稳健混合分析。

Conclusion: QTB 是一种五步决策架构，而非新的先验或兼容性统计量。它决定外部信息是不变地进入、需修复还是被排除，然后动态借用才作用于残余的经验兼容性。资格判定并不等于证明可交换性，降权也只是一道安全防线而非对残余偏倚的保证。

Abstract: PURPOSE: Dynamic Bayesian borrowing methods adapt the contribution of external information according to agreement or disagreement with current data. Qualify-Then-Borrow (QTB) makes explicit how scientific source assessment determines what external information reaches the borrowing model and when observed outcome agreement should determine borrowing strength.
  METHODS: QTB uses five steps: define the target; assess the external data; classify them as qualified, repairable, or not qualified; use them directly, repair them before use, or exclude them; and then apply the selected Bayesian borrowing method. Dynamic borrowing is the final step and operates on residual empirical compatibility after identified material differences have been addressed. We evaluated 82 prespecified scenarios with 10,000 replications each.
  RESULTS: Outcome data alone could not determine the appropriate borrowing decision. Two settings with the same external outcome distribution produced similar robust-mixture behavior but different QTB decisions because one had a known endpoint problem. When misclassified data were borrowed, full pooling reduced 95% coverage to 60.8%; robust-mixture downweighting improved coverage to 91.8% but left a -4.8 percentage-point bias. Repair of a measured population shift reduced absolute bias from 1.94 to 0.80 percentage points. Under strong negative residual drift, Type I error reached 10.0% despite downweighting. With fully compatible data, QTB reduced to the usual robust-mixture analysis.
  CONCLUSION: QTB is a five-step decision architecture, not a new prior or compatibility statistic. It determines whether external information proceeds unchanged, requires repair, or is excluded before dynamic borrowing operates on residual empirical compatibility. Qualification does not certify exchangeability, and downweighting is a safeguard rather than a guarantee against residual bias.

</details>


### [287] [Omitted Variable Bias in Difference-in-Differences Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19386)
*Juejue Wang,Pedro H. C. Sant'Anna,Victor Chernozhukov,Carlos Cinelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究差分中差分（DiD）设计中因不可观测混杂导致平行趋势假设不成立时的遗漏变量偏误（OVB）问题，给出ATT偏误的新刻画、多种量化混杂强度的方法、敏感性统计量及基于机器学习的高效推断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DiD设计依赖平行趋势假设，但该假设常因不可观测混杂而失效，导致ATT估计产生偏误。作者希望系统刻画这种偏误的来源与大小，并为实证研究者提供可操作的敏感性分析与推断工具。

Method: 作者推导了DiD中ATT的OVB公式，证明偏误主要由处理分配机制中的混杂强度决定，并提出三种量化方式：处理组中处理几率的平均变化、处理组与对照组间的混杂不平衡、以及未处理个体中处理几率被解释的变异。基于此构建敏感性统计量，并通过对观测协变量或前趋势的比较得到混杂强度的正式界限。最后利用现代机器学习算法进行灵活、高效的统计推断。

Result: 给出了ATT偏误的新颖OVB刻画，表明偏误主要受处理分配中混杂强度支配；提供了可常规报告的敏感性统计量（反转结论所需的最小混杂强度），以及基于观测协变量和前趋势的混杂强度正式界限，并配套灵活高效的推断方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法帮助研究者在平行趋势可能被违反时量化DiD结论的稳健性，并通过最低工资对青少年就业影响这一实证例子验证了方法的实用性。

Abstract: We study the omitted variable bias (OVB) problem in canonical difference-in-differences (DiD) designs when unobserved confounding induces departures from the parallel trends assumption. Our results provide a novel characterization of the OVB formula for the average treatment effect on the treated (ATT), which is of independent interest. We show how the ATT bias is mainly governed by the strength of confounding in the treatment assignment mechanism and provide alternative ways of quantifying this strength, such as (i) changes in the average odds of treatment among the treated, (ii) confounding imbalance between treated and control units, or (iii) variation explained in treatment odds among the untreated. Building on these results, we offer sensitivity statistics for routine reporting, describing the minimum strength of confounding required to overturn the conclusions of a DiD study, as well as formal bounds on the strength of confounders based on comparisons to observed covariates or pre-trends. Finally, we provide flexible and efficient statistical inference methods for the bounds on ATT, which can leverage modern machine learning algorithms for estimation. We demonstrate the utility of our approach in an empirical example that estimates the effects of minimum wage on teen employment.

</details>


### [288] [Bayesian Sample Size Determination: Sampling Distribution Estimation or Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19489)
*Luke Hagar,Paul Gustafson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从理论上推导了贝叶斯样本量确定方法的蒙特卡罗抽样分布，并通过大量数值研究评估了抽样分布估计法与探索法在偏差、精度和对调参敏感度方面的表现，以推动贝叶斯样本量确定的最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯研究中，样本量常需满足足够的功效或区间估计宽度要求，而对复杂模型确定最小样本量通常需要大量蒙特卡罗模拟。已有的两类策略——估计各样本量下事后总结量的抽样分布，或通过随机求根在样本量空间探索——孰优孰劣缺乏理论依据，因此需要从理论上刻画两者样本量推荐值的抽样分布并评估其性能。

Method: 作者从理论上推导了多种贝叶斯样本量确定方法下样本量推荐值的蒙特卡罗抽样分布；进而开展大规模数值研究，分别估计"抽样分布估计"策略与"抽样分布探索"策略（基于随机求根）的蒙特卡罗抽样分布，从偏差、精度以及对调参参数的敏感性三方面进行对比评估。

Result: 数值研究系统地估计了两类策略的蒙特卡罗抽样分布，揭示了它们在样本量推荐上的偏差大小、精度水平以及对调参参数的敏感性差异，为选择贝叶斯样本量确定方法提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 通过理论推导与数值实验，本文阐明了贝叶斯样本量确定中不同策略的统计特性，建议研究者依据对偏差、精度及调参敏感性的需求来选择合适方法，从而推广贝叶斯样本量确定的最佳实践。

Abstract: To design many Bayesian studies, the sample size is chosen to attain sufficient power to reject a null hypothesis or a desired probability that an interval estimate is sufficiently narrow. Determining the minimum suitable sample size for studies with complex models typically requires intensive Monte Carlo simulation. One simulation-based strategy to consider the sample-size space involves estimating sampling distributions of posterior summaries, such as posterior probabilities or interval estimate lengths, at various sample sizes. Another strategy instead explores these sampling distributions via stochastic root finding over the sample-size space, where the entire sampling distribution of posterior summaries is not estimated at any given sample size. The performance of these competing approaches can be assessed using their Monte Carlo sampling distributions of sample size recommendations across repeated implementations. In this paper, we theoretically derive these Monte Carlo sampling distributions for various Bayesian sample size determination approaches. To promote best practices for Bayesian sample size determination, we conduct extensive numerical studies to estimate these Monte Carlo sampling distributions for the sampling distribution estimation and exploration approaches to assess their bias, precision, and sensitivity to tuning parameters.

</details>


### [289] [Spatial Aggregation of ROC and Precision-Recall Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19517)
*Romain Pic,Zhongwei Zhang,Sebastian Engelke,Johanna Ziegel*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究空间预测场中ROC曲线与PR曲线的聚合策略，找出能同时保持预测优劣排序和曲线凹性/可实现性的聚合条件，提出两种满足要求的策略，并用AI全球天气预报实例说明不同聚合策略会导致不同的预测排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在天气预报中，预测结果以空间场形式给出，产生逐位置的ROC和PR曲线，通常需要聚合以便比较；但目前聚合策略对性能评估的影响尚不清楚，可能导致误导性解读。

Method: 作者系统分析不同ROC/PR曲线聚合策略，明确两个公平比较的理想性质（保持预测间的优劣排序、保持曲线的凹性或可实现性），推导出满足这些性质的充分条件，并提出两种满足条件的聚合策略，再与文献中已有策略进行对比。

Result: 得到保持主导关系和凹性/可实现性的充分条件，提出两种满足这些条件的聚合策略，并揭示现有策略可能产生误导的潜在陷阱。

Conclusion: 不同聚合策略会导致相互竞争的预测得到不同排名，因此提出实用指南以正确解读聚合后的ROC和PR曲线；用AI全球天气预报数据验证了框架的有效性。

Abstract: Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) and Precision-Recall (PR) curves are widely used to assess the discrimination ability of forecasts for binary events, such as threshold exceedances or warnings of extreme events. In weather forecasting, forecasts are provided as spatial fields, yielding location-wise ROC and PR curves that are often aggregated to facilitate comparison. However, the effect of the aggregation strategy on performance assessment remains poorly understood.
  We investigate how different aggregation strategies for ROC and PR curves affect the assessment of discrimination ability. In particular, we identify conditions under which aggregation strategies satisfy two desirable properties for fair comparison: preservation of dominance between forecasts and preservation of concavity or achievability of the curves. We obtain sufficient conditions and propose two strategies satisfying them. They are compared with existing strategies from the literature, and we analyze their properties and highlight potential pitfalls that may lead to misleading interpretations. Based on these findings, we provide practical guidelines for the interpretation of aggregated ROC and PR curves. The proposed framework is illustrated with AI-based global weather forecasts, showing how different aggregation strategies can yield different rankings of competing forecasts.

</details>


### [290] [Vector Vine Copula Models for Multivariate Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19547)
*Michael Stanley Smith,Lin Deng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出向量可绘藤（VD-vine）copula，将传统可绘藤copula从标量节点推广到向量节点，用于建模具有非高斯边缘、非线性动态及跨波次变化组成结构的多元纵向数据，并提供高效的似然评估、预测模拟及贝叶斯变分推断实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元纵向数据常呈现非高斯边缘分布、非线性动态依赖，且不同波次（wave）间的响应向量组成（维度/变量）会发生变化。传统标量可绘藤copula无法直接处理向量节点及波次间组成结构的不一致，因此需要一种能同时对向量边缘与序列依赖建模的向量copula框架。

Method: 提出VD-vine copula，以每个波次的响应向量作为多元边缘，通过一系列"连接向量copula"刻画序列依赖。推导递归的前向与后向条件传输（conditional transports），实现高效似然评估与预测模拟；在有限阶马尔可夫和平稳性约束下进行简约化约简。给出高斯与FGM连接向量copula的无约束参数化、灵活多元边缘，并采用贝叶斯变分推断进行实际估计。

Result: 模拟显示，当边缘分布非对称且序列依赖为多元时预测精度提升，而在正确设定的高斯面板向量自回归下损失很小。在包含1,093名个体、八个波次且响应向量各异的澳大利亚面板数据上，完整VD-vine在交叉验证分布预测中表现最佳，证明了捕捉非对称性与非线性依赖的价值。

Conclusion: VD-vine为处理非高斯、非线性、波次间组成变化的多元纵向数据提供了统一且灵活的建模框架，兼具高效计算与良好预测性能，在实证面板数据上优于所比较的其他模型，验证了建模非对称与非线性依赖的收益。

Abstract: Multivariate longitudinal data may exhibit non-Gaussian margins, nonlinear dynamics, and response vectors with composition that varies across waves. To account for these features, we introduce a vector drawable vine (VD-vine) copula that extends conventional drawable vine copulas from scalar to vector-valued nodes. Here, the response vector at each wave forms a multivariate marginal, and serial dependence is captured through a sequence of linking vector copulas. We establish that the VD-vine is itself a vector copula and reduces to a conventional drawable vine for scalar nodes. Recursive forward and backward conditional transports are derived that enable efficient likelihood evaluation and predictive simulation, with parsimonious reductions under finite-order Markov and stationary restrictions. Unconstrained parameterizations for Gaussian and FGM linking vector copulas, flexible multivariate marginals, and Bayesian variational inference provide a practical implementation. Simulations show improved predictive accuracy when the marginals are asymmetric and serial dependence is multivariate, with little loss under a correctly specified Gaussian panel vector autoregression. In an eight-wave Australian panel of 1,093 individuals with varying response vectors, the full VD-vine delivers the best cross-validated distributional forecasts among the models considered, establishing the benefit of capturing asymmetry and nonlinear dependence.

</details>


### [291] [Oracle high-dimensional $M$-estimation using smooth reparameterization for sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19558)
*Yoichi Nishiyama*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的非线性正则化框架，通过光滑的重参数化稀疏映射 RePS 将稀疏性直接嵌入参数变换，用于高维 M 估计（线性模型与 Cox 比例风险模型），并以 λ‖β‖₁ 作为惩罚项，实现普适的 oracle 等价性与无偏 oracle 渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加性非凸惩罚（如 SCAD、MCP）在高维 M 估计中存在严重的优化困难，需要一种既能自适应局部参数尺度、抑制高维噪声，又能在极限下恢复无偏 oracle 渐近正态性的统一框架。

Method: 构建统一非线性正则化框架，引入光滑（C²）逐分量"稀疏重参数化映射 RePS"φ^(ν)，使物理参数满足 θ=φ^(ν)(β)，惩罚项取 λ‖β‖₁（而非 λ‖θ‖₁）；该结构随局部参数尺度动态调整。运用 Primal-Dual Witness（原始-对偶见证）方法，在维度依赖的对数标度 n^{-1/2} ≪ ν ≤ log p 下建立线性模型与 Cox 模型统一的 oracle 等价性，并显式容纳生存分析中的平移不变性等模型结构。

Result: 广泛的蒙特卡洛模拟表明：在高维线性模型中该框架持续实现优越的假阳性控制与高 95% 置信区间覆盖率；在 Cox 比例风险模型中整体表现与 SCAD、MCP 等最先进方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结构化的非线性重参数化代替传统非凸惩罚，在两类高维模型间统一实现 oracle 等价与无偏渐近推断，兼具优良的优化性质与更优的统计性能。

Abstract: This paper establishes a unified non-linear regularization framework for high-dimensional $M$-estimation, encompassing both linear models and Cox's proportional hazards models. Rather than relying on traditional additive non-convex penalties which pose severe optimization challenges, the proposed paradigm embeds sparsity directly into the transformation for the physical parameter $θ=φ^{(ν)}(β)$ using a smooth ($C^2$) component-wise "ReParametrization map for Sparsity (RePS)" $φ^{(ν)}$, and the penalty term is $λ\Vert β\Vert_1$ rather than $λ\Vert θ\Vert_1$. This structural formulation dynamically adapts to local parameter scales, suppressing high-dimensional noise while simultaneously recovering unbiased oracle asymptotic normality under the large-sample limit. Through the Primal-Dual Witness method, we establish a unified oracle equivalence for both model classes under a dimension-dependent logarithmic scaling $n^{-1/2} \ll ν\leq \log p$, explicitly accommodating model-specific structures such as the shift-invariance in survival analysis. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves superior false-positive control and high 95% confidence interval coverage in high-dimensional linear models, while also delivering performance comparable or superior in all aspects to state-of-the-art methods like SCAD and MCP in proportional hazards models.

</details>


### [292] [Selective Inference in Growth Curve Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19573)
*Haochen Lei,Qian Zhang,Hongyuan Cao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据分裂（data-fission）的增长曲线模型选择后推断框架，通过加减高斯噪声将同一批数据的信息拆分到选择与推断两个阶段，既保留所有样本又保证推断有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增长曲线模型广泛应用于心理学研究，变量选择有助于识别与纵向异质性相关的基线特征。但传统上数据驱动的变量选择与后续推断共用同一批结局数据，会导致推断失效（选择偏差），因此需要一种能保证选择后推断有效性的方法。

Method: 作者构建了数据分裂框架：通过对结局数据加上和减去独立高斯噪声，将样本信息同时用于选择阶段和推断阶段，且两个阶段均保留全部被试。框架支持灵活的变量选择程序，并以所选工作模型中的协方差加权线性投影参数作为推断目标。当协方差结构已知时给出精确推断；当协方差结构需估计时，在适当正则条件下证明了渐近有效性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法能提供有效的推断，且相比被试水平的简单数据分割具有更高的效率；而朴素的（未校正的）选择后推断则存在偏倚。

Conclusion: 该数据分裂框架为增长曲线模型中的选择后推断提供了有效且高效的解决方案，并通过美国青年纵向研究（Longitudinal Study of American Youth）的数据应用加以说明。

Abstract: Growth curve models are widely used in psychological research, and variable selection can help identify baseline characteristics associated with longitudinal heterogeneity. However, conventional inference after data-driven variable selection can be invalid because the same outcome data are used for both selection and inference. We develop a data-fission framework for post-selection inference in growth-curve models that separates the information used for selection and inference while retaining all participants in both stages through the addition and subtraction of Gaussian noise. The framework accommodates flexible variable-selection procedures and targets covariance-weighted linear projection parameters in the selected working model. It provides exact inference when the covariance structure is known, and we establish asymptotic validity under suitable regularity conditions when the covariance structure is estimated. Simulations show that the proposed method provides valid inference and improves efficiency over subject-level data splitting, whereas naïve post-selection inference can be biased. An application to the Longitudinal Study of American Youth illustrates the proposed framework.

</details>


### [293] [Heterogeneity-calibrated Byzantine-robust distributed composite quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19701)
*Xiaofei Wu,Jian Qing Shi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式环境下稀疏复合分位数回归（CQR），针对异构诚实节点与拜占庭节点的混合数据提出 HC-RCQR 方法：诚实节点本地剖分截距并用近似逆剖面 Hessian 校准得分，服务器通过坐标向修剪与软阈值更新估计；理论给出收缩与支持恢复保证，并通过仿真和共享单车需求数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式数据中节点可能异构（协变量分布、误差分布、分位截距不同），且存在可能发送任意向量的拜占庭节点，传统方法在异构性与对抗性污染下难以稳健估计共同的稀疏斜率，且非校准得分易受中间拜占庭报告影响。

Method: 提出异质性校准的稳健 CQR（HC-RCQR）：诚实节点对本地截距做剖面（profiling），利用近似逆剖面 Hessian 校准得分向量后上传；服务器对接收向量进行坐标向修剪（coordinatewise trimming）和软阈值处理以更新估计；结合一个标量示例揭示非等曲率下中间拜占庭报告如何存活及理想校准的抑制效果。

Result: 标量示例表明当诚实节点曲率不等时，中间拜占庭报告可绕过修剪，理想校准可压缩其影响；还证明了在故障身份未知时非限制诚实节点斜率的均值不可识别；在适当条件下建立条件收缩与支持恢复保证，误差界将得分偏移、抽样波动、污染、校准误差与牛顿余项分离。

Conclusion: HC-RCQR 通过异质性校准与坐标向稳健聚合，能在异构分布式数据与拜占庭攻击下有效估计稀疏斜率，理论收缩与支持恢复保证及数值实验验证了方法的稳健性与实用性，校准对抑制中间拜占庭报告、保障识别性至关重要。

Abstract: We study sparse composite quantile regression (CQR) for distributed data with heterogeneous honest sites and Byzantine workers. Honest sites share a common slope but may differ in their covariate distributions, error laws, and quantile intercepts. The proposed heterogeneity-calibrated robust CQR (HC-RCQR) profiles local intercepts and calibrates scores using an approximate inverse profile Hessian. Honest workers transmit the resulting vectors, whereas Byzantine workers may send arbitrary vectors. The server updates the estimate by coordinatewise trimming and soft thresholding. A scalar example shows how unequal honest-site curvatures allow intermediate Byzantine reports to survive trimming and how ideal calibration reduces their possible effect. We also establish nonidentification of the mean of unrestricted honest-site slopes when fault identities are unknown. Under suitable conditions, we establish conditional contraction and support-recovery guarantees. The bound separates score offset, sampling fluctuation, contamination, calibration error, and the Newton remainder. Simulations and a bike-demand study examine performance under heterogeneous data and adversarial messages.

</details>


### [294] [Report resolution in federated multiple testing under family-wise error control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19708)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在无法共享原始数据、只能交换p值量化报告的多机构场景下，家庭错误率（FWER）控制框架中的功效损失。核心结论是：功效损失源于报告的分辨率（压缩），而非去中心化本身；在正则性与功效目标条件下，任何有限分辨率都会损失功效，且最优损失随分辨率平方反比衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个机构需要在家庭错误率控制下联合检验一组假设，但因数据无法汇总，只能由各站点对每个假设发布其自身p值的报告。报告的"分辨率"（可取值数量）受限，作者希望量化这种报告机制相对于最优集中式程序（oracle）所损失的功效，并找到最优的报告与合并规则。

Method: 将问题形式化为：在给定报告分辨率约束下，选择最优的报告（p值的离散化/压缩）与合并规则，使其功效最大化。通过分析有限分辨率划分的功效上界，并借助两个站点的Beta分布算例进行数值验证。关键结论包括：能恢复p值的报告无损功效；等宽区间划分在阶数上达到最优；对给定报告给出功效最优的合并规则，并提出仅依赖确切已知零分布、且在任意跨假设相关下有效的合并规则。

Result: 在所述正则性与功效目标条件下：任何有限分辨率都会失去功效；最优损失随分辨率的平方反比衰减（即 O(1/R²)，R为分辨率），等宽区间达到该阶数，且不存在相同单元数的其他划分能改进此阶数。两站点Beta算例中，单假设下一比特优化报告近似无损，但两个假设时功效损失数倍于单假设情形。在水平0.05、两个Beta(1,2)假设下，最优合并的三比特等宽报告可保留99.2%的oracle功效。

Conclusion: 功效损失根源于p值压缩（有限分辨率）而非去中心化；损失随分辨率平方反比衰减是可达到的最优阶。等宽区间是阶数最优的划分。低比特报告在单假设下几乎无损，但多假设下损失加剧；三比特报告即可在示例中保留超99%的功效，并可通过依赖任意相关的零分布规则实现。

Abstract: Several institutions test one family of hypotheses under family-wise error control but cannot pool their data, so each site releases, for each hypothesis, only a report of its own p-value. The report's resolution is the number of values it can take. We quantify the power lost to such reports relative to the most powerful centralized procedure (the oracle), with reports and combining rule chosen optimally. A report from which the p-value can be recovered loses no power, so the loss is due to compression, not decentralization. Under the stated regularity and power-objective conditions, every finite resolution loses power, and the optimal loss decays as the inverse square of the resolution: equal-width intervals attain this order, and no partition into as many cells improves it. In two-site Beta examples, optimized one-bit reports are nearly lossless for a single hypothesis but lose several times as much power for two. At level 0.05 with two Beta(1, 2) hypotheses, optimally combined three-bit equal-width reports retain 99.2% of oracle power. We give the power-optimal combining rule for given reports, and a rule using only their exactly known null distribution, valid under arbitrary dependence across hypotheses.

</details>


### [295] [Matrix Graphical Model Via Joint Estimation of Partial Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19718)
*Hyewon Kim,Seongoh Park*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对矩阵图模型中的条件依赖结构估计问题，提出了一种在统一优化框架下联合估计偏相关系数的方法，以克服现有回归类方法导致的不对称性和调参困难，并在模拟与肺结核蛋白质表达数据上验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回归的矩阵图模型图估计方法通过多个独立的惩罚回归问题实现，这自然导致估计出的图结构不对称，且涉及多个调参问题、选择最优模型困难。作者希望提出一种能同时估计所有偏相关系数、保持对称性并简化模型选择的统一方法。

Method: 在可分离协方差（精度矩阵为 Kronecker 乘积）的矩阵图模型框架下，提出联合估计偏相关系数的方法：将所有偏相关系数纳入一个统一的优化框架中同时估计，从而保持行、列图结构的对称性，并缓解调参难度。

Result: 数值研究表明，所提方法相比现有方法显著提升了图恢复性能。在肺结核患者蛋白质表达数据的实际分析中，该方法成功比较了两组患者的蛋白质网络，并恢复出时间依赖结构。

Conclusion: 通过联合估计偏相关系数，所提方法在保持对称性、简化调参的同时提升了矩阵图模型的图估计精度，并在真实蛋白质表达数据上展现出实用价值。

Abstract: Matrix graphical models aim to characterize conditional dependence structures in matrix-variate data under a separable covariance assumption. In this framework, the precision matrix is decomposed as a Kronecker product, enabling separate modeling of undirected graphs across row and column domains. Existing methods have been developed for this problem, including likelihood-based approaches and regression-based procedures for graph estimation. Likelihood-based methods estimate precision matrices directly and recover graph structures indirectly, whereas regression-based approaches directly target estimating edges among variables, thus outperforming the former. However, existing regression-based methods are based on multiple penalized regression problems, which naturally yields asymmetry in estimated graphs and computational difficulty in selecting tuning parameters. To address the limitations, we propose a joint estimation of partial correlations in matrix graphical models. The proposed method estimates all partial correlations simultaneously within a unified optimization framework, thereby preserving symmetry and easing the pain of selecting the best models. Numerical studies demonstrate that the proposed method improves graph recovery performance compared to existing approaches. We also analyze protein expression data collected from patients with pulmonary tuberculosis, measured repeatedly at multiple time points, where the proposed method compares protein networks between two groups of patients and recovers the temporal dependence structure.

</details>


### [296] [Mutual Information as a Tool for Optimal Classification: Application to Identifying Rapid-Responding Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19781)
*Santeri Holopainen,Jari Metsämuuronen,Mikko-Jussi Laakso,Janne V. Kujala*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于互信息的非参数框架，用于在大规模测评中识别快速作答行为，避免传统方法对总体的参数假设，并在PISA 2022数学数据上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别大规模测评中快速作答行为的方法依赖于对总体的参数假设，限制了其适用性和稳健性，因此需要一种无需参数假设的替代方案。

Method: 作者提出一种新颖的、基于互信息的非参数方法框架。该框架计算观测作答与离散化作答时间之间的互信息，并通过最大化信息增益来确定区分快速作答与投入作答的阈值。框架内各方法仅在相关变量的类别数量上有所不同，文中明确给出三种方法：方法一使用作答正确性与二值化作答时间；方法二使用正确性并将时间划分为三组；方法三使用原始作答与二值化时间。

Result: 将上述方法应用于2022年国际学生评估项目（PISA）采集的数学成就数据。此外，在总体层面和实现（realised）层面的若干现实条件下考察了方法一的行为与可用性。结果表明，所提框架是识别快速作答的一种可行替代方案。

Conclusion: 该框架的新颖之处在于其非参数特性，以及能够利用原始作答而非仅依赖正确性的能力。最后，作者讨论了该主题未来可能的研究方向。

Abstract: Existing methods for identifying rapid-responding behaviour in large-scale assessments require parametric assumptions about the population. In this study, we propose a novel, non-parametric, mutual information-based framework of methods as an alternative. The methods within this framework compute the mutual information of the observed responses and discretised response times and maximise the information gain to determine a threshold that differentiates rapid responses from engaged responses. The only difference between the methods is the number of categories in the relevant variables. We present three methods explicitly. The first method uses response correctness and binarised response times. The second method uses correctness and categorises time into three groups. The third method uses raw responses and binarised times. We applied these methods to mathematics achievement data collected through the Programme for International Student Assessment in 2022. Furthermore, we examined the behaviour and usability of the first method in certain realistic conditions at the population and realised levels. The results indicated that the proposed framework is a viable alternative for identifying rapid responses. The framework's novelty lies in its non-parametric nature and its ability to utilise raw responses instead of correctness. Finally, we discuss some possible future research directions on this topic.

</details>


### [297] [Multi-Absorbing Phase-Type Distributions for Right-Censored Competing Risks Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19921)
*Zhihao Qiao,Budhi Surya,Azam Asanjarani,Yoni Nazarathy*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出用多吸收态相位型（MAPH）分布进行竞争风险建模，并开发了可处理精确事件时间与右删失观测的 EM 算法，配套提供两个 Julia 软件包，在重症监护住院时长数据上取得了优于既有方法的似然值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位型（PH）分布是寿命时长建模的灵活半参数工具，但在竞争风险场景下如何纳入删失数据（尤其是仅知道“存活至删失时刻”而不知最终事件原因的被试）尚缺乏完整的推断方法与软件实现。作者旨在为竞争风险中的多吸收态相位型分布建立完整的方法学和可复现的软件支持。

Method: 作者首先给出多吸收态相位型（MAPH）分布的基本性质，该分布同时记录吸收时间及其原因；随后开发 EM 算法，用于从精确事件时间/原因观测及独立右删失观测中进行参数推断。对删失个体，E 步以“存活至删失时刻”为条件，补齐潜在瞬态路径及其最终吸收原因，并通过矩阵指数得到按原因分解的充分统计量的闭式期望。

Result: 方法在应用与数值性质上得到验证，并配套实现于两个 Julia 软件包（涵盖精确事件与删失两种情形的 E 步）。将方法应用于完整的重症监护（ICU）住院时长数据（包含删失记录）时，其似然值高于此前已发表的对同一批数据的相位型竞争风险拟合结果。

Conclusion: MAPH 分布在竞争风险建模中具有可行性与优越性，所提出的 EM 算法及 Julia 软件能有效利用删失信息、提升拟合优度，为生存与可靠性分析提供实用工具。

Abstract: Phase-type (PH) distributions are versatile semi-parametric models for lifetime duration and can be used in survival and reliability analysis. In this paper we put forward methods and software for using PH distributions in a competing-risks model. The resulting multi-absorbing phase-type (MAPH) distribution records both the time until absorption and its cause. After formulating basic properties of this family, we develop an EM algorithm for parameter inference from exact event-time/cause observations and independently right-censored observations. For a censored subject, the E-step conditions on survival up to the censoring time and imputes both the latent transient path and its eventual cause of absorption; closed-form cause-resolved sufficient-statistic expectations are obtained from matrix exponentials. We illustrate applications and numerical properties and describe two accompanying Julia packages, in which both the exact-event and the censored E-step are implemented. Applied to intensive-care length-of-stay data in full, censored records included, the method attains a higher likelihood than the phase-type competing-risks fit previously published for those data.

</details>


### [298] [Gain-function optimisation of graphical multiple testing procedures for confirmatory clinical trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19994)
*Alexander D. V. Spiers,Michael J. Grayling,Graham M. Wheeler,Adrian P. Mander*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于多重检验问题的图形化程序选择框架：通过设定显式的“增益函数”来量化不同假设拒绝组合对申办方的价值，并选择使期望增益最大化的检验图，同时保持族错误率（FWER）控制。该框架还可处理效应量与相关性不确定性及成组序贯设计中尽早获得标签声明的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形化多重检验程序虽能灵活透明地控制FWER，但实践中如何选择检验图缺乏系统方法，申办方常退回到固定序列或Holm程序，而这些程序可能无法准确反映试验真正要达成的目标（如多个标签声明、监管门槛等）。

Method: 提出选择使显式增益函数最大化的检验图：增益函数刻画每种可能被拒绝的假设集合对申办方的价值（通常在通过监管门槛前无价值，之后每增加一个声明获得增量价值）。搜索被限定在图形化程序范围内以保证FWER控制。文中给出从临床、商业与监管团队成员中结构化征求（elicitation）增益函数的流程；通过在对效应量与相关性的可能取值集合上平均性能来处理设计阶段的不确定性；对成组序贯设计还提出对早期声明给予额外奖励的方法。

Result: 通过五个源自真实或假设的制药试验案例，表明最优检验图取决于试验成功的定义方式、设计假设的不确定程度，以及在成组序贯设定下声明能够成立的时间。传统功效准则被证明是该框架的特例。

Conclusion: 以增益函数为导向选择图形化检验程序，能够更贴切地反映试验真实目标，同时保证FWER控制，并为多标签、不确定性及早期声明等实际场景提供了系统、透明的决策框架。

Abstract: Graphical multiple testing procedures are a flexible and transparent way to control the family-wise error rate when a confirmatory trial pursues several label claims, but they leave open the question of which graph to use. In practice sponsors often fall back on fixed-sequence or Holm procedures that may poorly reflect what the trial is actually trying to achieve. We propose to instead choose the graph that maximises an explicit gain function, which states what each possible set of rejected hypotheses is worth to the sponsor: typically nothing until a regulatory hurdle is cleared, then an incremental amount for each further claim. Conventional power criteria are recovered as special cases, and because the search is confined to graphical procedures, family-wise error rate control holds whichever graph is selected. We set out a structured procedure for eliciting the gain function from the clinical, commercial, and regulatory members of a trial team. The proposed framework also handles uncertainty in the treatment effects and correlations assumed at the design stage, by averaging performance over their plausible values rather than fixing a single assumption. For group sequential designs, we describe how it can further reward early claims. Five examples, drawn from real and hypothetical pharmaceutical trials, show that the best graph depends on how trial success is defined, how uncertain the design assumptions are, and, in a group sequential setting, when claims can be established.

</details>


### [299] [A Bayesian Bi-Directional Splitting Framework for Variable Selection in Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20000)
*Aaron Coats,Vinny Davies,Mayetri Gupta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大规模表格数据（行与列同时庞大）的可扩展贝叶斯变量选择框架，采用沿两个方向分批的“分而治之”策略，各批次独立并行分析后通过两阶段共识过程合并结果，在保证变量选择性能的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表格数据在样本数（行）和协变量数（列）上都日益庞大，给贝叶斯变量选择带来巨大计算负担。现有文献多集中于单一维度（大样本量或高维协变量）的扩展，而缺乏在行、列同时很大且面向实际应用的条件下兼顾可扩展性的贝叶斯变量选择方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的贝叶斯变量选择框架：采用分而治之方法，将数据沿行和列两个方向切分成批次，各批次独立并行分析，随后通过一个两阶段共识（consensus）过程将各批次结果合并整合。

Result: 实验表明该方法在获得计算收益的同时保持了较强的变量选择性能，即便因数据切分造成信息损失，仍能在带噪、高维场景下成功识别相关信号。文章还给出调参选择与数据划分策略等实用指南，并在H3N2流感数据集上进行了实际应用。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了行、列同时大规模时贝叶斯变量选择的计算瓶颈，通过二维分批并行与两阶段共识合并，在效率与选择准确性之间取得良好平衡，并提供了可操作的实践建议与真实数据验证。

Abstract: Modern tabular datasets are becoming increasingly large, both in the number of samples and covariates, posing significant challenges for Bayesian variable selection due to the resulting computational burden. While there is extensive literature on scaling Bayesian inference to large numbers of observations or high-dimensional covariate spaces, comparatively little work addresses scalable Bayesian variable selection when both dimensions are large simultaneously in a practical setting. This paper presents a novel Bayesian variable selection framework for efficiently analysing data with a large number of both rows and columns. The proposed framework operates via a divide-and-conquer approach, splitting data into batches along both directions and analysing each batch independently in parallel, after which the results are combined together in a two-phase consensus procedure. Experiments demonstrate the computational gains while retaining strong variable selection performance, successfully identifying relevant signals in noisy, high-dimensional settings despite the loss of information induced by data splitting. Practical guidelines are also provided, including recommendations for tuning parameter choices and effective data partitioning strategies, followed by a real application to an H3N2 influenza dataset.

</details>


### [300] [Parameter Estimation for the Mixed Fractional Merton Jump Diffusion Model with EM Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20041)
*Chidiogo Joy Agboeke,Hamidreza Maleki Almani,Dario Gasbarra,Foad Shokrollahi,Tommi Sottinen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Metropolis–Hastings采样的期望最大化（EM）算法，用于混合分数阶Merton跳扩散模型的参数估计，该模型同时刻画金融收益的长记忆性与跳变行为，并在赫尔辛基股指数据上验证了方法的可靠性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列往往同时呈现长期依赖（长记忆性）与突发跳变两类特征，而传统模型难以兼顾。现有估计方法在跳扩散模型与分数阶布朗运动结合时面临潜变量推断与参数识别的困难，因此需要一种能同时处理长记忆和跳变、且计算上可行的估计框架。

Method: 该文构建混合分数阶Merton跳扩散模型：用分数阶布朗运动刻画长程依赖，用复合泊松跳过程描述收益的突变。参数估计采用EM算法：E步利用Metropolis–Hastings（马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛）采样推断潜在跳过程；M步通过最大化期望完整数据似然更新模型参数。此外，在适当正则条件下建立了估计量的一致性与渐近正态性。

Result: 所提估计量被证明在合适正则条件下具有一致性与渐近正态性。将该方法应用于赫尔辛基股指（OMXH25），实证显示该估计框架能可靠、计算高效地拟合兼具长记忆依赖与跳变行为的金融时间序列。

Conclusion: 提出的EM-MH估计框架为长记忆性与跳变行为并存的金融时间序列建模提供了可靠且计算高效的参数估计方法，具有一定的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes an Expectation--Maximization algorithm with Metropolis--Hastings sampling for parameter estimation in a Mixed Fractional Merton Jump Diffusion model. The model combines fractional Brownian motion to capture long-range dependence with a compound Poisson jump process to describe abrupt movements in financial returns. The latent jump process is inferred during the E-step using Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, while the M-step updates the model parameters by maximizing the expected complete-data likelihood. The consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed estimator are established under suitable regularity conditions. The methodology is applied to the Helsinki Stock Index (OMXH25). The proposed estimation framework provides a reliable and computationally efficient approach for modeling financial time series exhibiting both long-memory dependence and jump behavior.

</details>


### [301] [Noise-adjusted turnover in estimated networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20044)
*Sultan Amed,Sayantan Banerjee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究两个时点网络的边集变化估计问题，指出观测到的汉明周转量既包含真实的网络结构重连，也包含图选择误差；在齐次边误分类模型下可给出潜在周转量的闭式无偏调整，并分析稀疏性、校准误差和跨期相关性对调整偏差的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济网络通常分两期分别估计，边集的变化被解读为结构性重连，但两期网络都是估计得到的，观测周转量会混入图选择误差，导致对真实重连的推断失真，因此需要对误差来源进行识别与校正。

Method: 在齐次边误分类模型下，假设已知敏感度与特异度，且两期估计边指示变量条件独立，推导出基于观测周转量和两期估计图规模的潜在周转量的闭式无偏调整公式；进而分析稀疏性、误分类概率校准误差及跨期依赖对调整结果的偏差影响，并给出相对网络规模一致性的充分条件。

Result: 当真实链接数与网络节点数 p 成比例时，阶为 p^{-1} 的假阳性概率会产生阶为 p 的期望虚假周转量；对假阳性概率的校准若存在同阶误差，调整后仍会留下阶为 p 的偏差；文章还推导了跨期相关引入的偏差，并提供了相对网络规模一致性的充分条件，数值实验验证了有限样本下的结论。

Conclusion: 两快照网络的边集周转量必须经过误分类误差调整才能反映真实结构重连；稀疏性放大校准误差的负面影响，跨期依赖同样会引入系统偏差，仅在满足一定条件时估计量才能随网络规模保持一致。

Abstract: Economic networks are often estimated separately over two periods, and changes in their edge sets are interpreted as structural rewiring. Since both networks are estimated, observed turnover also reflects graph-selection error. We study the two-snapshot Hamming-turnover functional under a homogeneous edge-misclassification model. With known sensitivity and specificity and conditional independence of the estimated edge indicators across periods, latent turnover admits a closed-form unbiased adjustment based only on observed turnover and the two estimated graph sizes. We then examine the effects of sparsity, calibration error and dependence across periods. When the number of true links is proportional to $p$, a false-positive probability of order $p^{-1}$ generates expected spurious turnover of order $p$. An error of the same order in calibrating the false-positive probability can likewise leave order-$p$ bias after adjustment. We also derive the bias induced by cross-period dependence and give sufficient conditions for consistency relative to network size. Numerical results illustrate the finite-sample implications.

</details>


### [302] [Identification and Estimation of Causal Estimands with Missing Not at Random Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20113)
*Faria Rauf Ria,Tarikul Islam,Mahbub A. H. M. Latif*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对缺失非随机（MNAR）数据下的因果推断与中介分析问题，利用完备性条件建立可识别性结果，并基于EM算法提出估计方法，模拟和NHANES实际数据均验证了其优于完全案例分析和多重插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当混杂因素和结局同时存在部分缺失且缺失机制为MNAR时，仅靠因果推断所需的常规假设无法识别因果估计量，现有处理缺失数据的方法（如完全案例分析和多重插补）在MNAR下会产生较大偏差，因此需要发展新的可识别性与估计框架。

Method: 作者在若干合理的MNAR机制下，借助完备性（completeness）条件建立平均处理效应（ATE）的可识别性结果；在此基础上提出基于期望最大化（EM）算法的估计方法；并将该识别与估计框架进一步推广到中介分析，用于估计自然直接效应和自然间接效应。

Result: 大量模拟研究表明，在所考虑的MNAR机制下，所提方法的偏差显著低于完全案例分析和多重插补两种常用方法。在NHANES数据应用中，该方法被用于估计教育对抑郁的因果效应（以健康状况为中介变量）。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于完备性条件和EM算法的框架能够在MNAR数据下有效识别和估计平均处理效应及自然直接/间接效应，相比传统缺失数据处理方法具有更低偏差，并成功应用于NHANES实际数据分析。

Abstract: Missing not at random (MNAR) data pose significant challenges for causal inference, particularly when both confounders and the outcome are partially observed. Without additional assumptions beyond those required for causal inference, causal estimands are generally not identifiable under MNAR mechanisms. This paper first develops identification results for the causal estimand, the average treatment effect, under several plausible MNAR mechanisms using completeness conditions, and proposes an estimation approach based on the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. We further extend this identification and estimation framework to mediation analysis, enabling the estimation of natural direct and indirect effects under MNAR mechanisms. Through extensive simulation studies, we compare the proposed method with two widely used approaches for handling missing data, complete-case analysis and multiple imputation. The results show that the proposed method yields substantially lower bias under the considered MNAR mechanisms. Finally, we apply the proposed approach to NHANES data to estimate the causal effect of education on depression, with health condition as the mediator.

</details>


### [303] [Exponential Smoothing for Time Series of Random Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20274)
*Takuo Matsubara,Peiwen Jiang,Wilson Ye Chen,Minh-Ngoc Tran*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出"测地指数平滑"(geodesic exponential smoothing)，将指数平滑推广到Hadamard空间中的随机对象时间序列(如协方差矩阵、概率分布、函数型数据)，仅用一个标量参数即可实现在线、非平稳条件下的预测，并给出参数估计的一致性与真实数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对象(协方差矩阵、概率分布、函数型数据)构成的时间序列无法依赖标准算术运算，现有对象值时间序列的自回归模型结构较重、需假设平稳性且参数多；需要一种不依赖加法/标量乘法的轻量、在线、非平稳的预测方法。

Method: 在Hadamard空间中定义测地指数平滑：预测水平沿测地线向每个新观测移动一个固定比例；平滑参数通过最小化观测与预测之间的平均平方距离来估计。进一步引入创新机制(innovations mechanism)，使每个观测在给定当前水平的条件下其Fréchet均值等于当前水平，构成度量空间中的创新状态空间模型；利用Hadamard空间的拟线性化(quasilinearization)分析生成过程的样本路径性质。

Result: 在该创新机制下，证明了生成过程的样本路径性质，并证明了平滑参数估计量的几乎必然一致性(almost-sure consistency)。三个真实数据应用(协方差矩阵、分布型、函数型时间序列)表明，该方法相比结构更重的替代模型的预测性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 测地指数平滑为Hadamard空间中的对象值时间序列提供了一种仅含单一标量参数、无需平稳假设、每观测常数时间在线更新的预测框架，理论基础(一致性与样本路径性质)与实证表现均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Time series of random objects, such as covariance matrices, probability distributions, and functional data, call for forecasting methods that do not rely on standard arithmetic operations. We introduce geodesic exponential smoothing, a generalization of exponential smoothing to time series in Hadamard spaces: the forecast level moves a fixed fraction of the way along the geodesic toward each new observation. The smoothing parameter is estimated by minimizing the average squared distance between observations and their forecasts. We further introduce an innovations mechanism under which each observation has conditional Fréchet mean equal to the current level, providing the metric-space analog of the innovations state-space model.
